
// ==========================================
// File: CursorManager.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

public class CursorManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static CursorManager Instance { get; private set; }

    [Header("Settings")]
    public bool lockOnStart = true;     // 시작 시 자동 락
    public bool escToggle = true;       // ESC로 토글 허용(디버그/메뉴용)

    void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
        {
            Destroy(gameObject);
            return;
        }
        Instance = this;
        DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);

        if (lockOnStart) Lock();
    }

    void Update()
    {
        if (!escToggle) return;

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape))
        {
            if (Cursor.lockState == CursorLockMode.Locked) Unlock();
            else Lock();
        }
    }

    public static void Lock()
    {
        Cursor.lockState = CursorLockMode.Locked;
        Cursor.visible = false;
    }

    public static void Unlock()
    {
        Cursor.lockState = CursorLockMode.None;
        Cursor.visible = true;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: AnimationEventReceiver.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

/// ?좊땲硫붿씠???대깽???섏떊湲?媛???⑤쭅/?쇳럺?명쉶??怨듦꺽 ?뚮옒洹?
/// - ?대┰ ?대깽?몄슜 硫붿꽌??
///   ParryWindow_Pulse / ParryWindow_Open / ParryWindow_Close
///   PerfectDodgeWindow_Pulse([seconds]) / PerfectDodgeWindow_Open([seconds]) / PerfectDodgeWindow_Close
///   AE_AttackStart / AE_AttackEnd
/// - ?쇳럺?명쉶??李쎌? 臾댁씤??float ?몄옄 紐⑤몢 ?덉슜(?대┰ ?대깽???몄쓽)
[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public sealed class AnimationEventReceiver : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("李몄“(媛숈? 罹먮┃???ㅻ툕?앺듃 ??")]
    [Tooltip("媛???⑤쭅 ?먯젙 而⑦듃濡ㅻ윭")]
    [SerializeField] private PlayerGuardController guard;
    [Tooltip("?쇳럺???뚰뵾 ?먯젙 而⑦듃濡ㅻ윭")]
    [SerializeField] private PerfectDodgeController perfectDodge;
    [Tooltip("?좊땲硫붿씠??怨듦꺽 ?뚮옒洹??숆린?붿슜)")]
    [SerializeField] private Animator anim;

    [Header("?좊땲硫붿씠???뚮씪誘명꽣紐?)]
    [Tooltip("怨듦꺽 以??щ? Bool ?뚮씪誘명꽣紐??? IsAttacking)")]
    [SerializeField] private string attackingBoolParam = "IsAttacking";

    // ?대? ?곹깭
    private int _attackingHash;
    private bool _hasAttackingBool;

    // ?쇳럺???뚰뵾 李?湲곕낯 吏?띿떆媛??대┰ ?대깽?몄뿉 ?몄옄瑜????섍릿 寃쎌슦)
    private const float DefaultPerfectDodgeWindow = 0.16f;

    private void Reset()      => TryAutoWire();
    private void Awake()
    {
        TryAutoWire();
        _attackingHash = Animator.StringToHash(attackingBoolParam);
        _hasAttackingBool = HasBool(anim, attackingBoolParam);
        if (!_hasAttackingBool)
            Debug.LogWarning($"[AnimEventReceiver] Animator Bool '{attackingBoolParam}' 瑜?李얠? 紐삵뻽?듬땲?? AE_AttackStart/End??臾댁떆?⑸땲??", this);
    }

#if UNITY_EDITOR
    private void OnValidate()
    {
        // ?먮뵒?곗뿉??李몄“ 鍮좎?硫??먮룞 ?곌껐 ?쒕룄
        TryAutoWire();
        _attackingHash = Animator.StringToHash(attackingBoolParam);
    }
#endif

    private void TryAutoWire()
    {
        if (anim == null)        anim = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>(true) ?? GetComponent<Animator>();
        if (guard == null)       guard = GetComponentInParent<PlayerGuardController>(true);
        if (perfectDodge == null)perfectDodge = GetComponentInParent<PerfectDodgeController>(true);
    }

    private static bool HasBool(Animator a, string name)
    {
        if (a == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) return false;
        foreach (var p in a.parameters)
            if (p.type == AnimatorControllerParameterType.Bool && p.name == name)
                return true;
        return false;
    }

    // ===================== ?⑤쭅 李?=====================
    // ?대┰ ?대깽?몄뿉??吏곸젒 ?몄텧
    public void ParryWindow_Pulse()  => guard?.OpenParryWindow();
    public void ParryWindow_Open()   => guard?.OpenParryWindow();
    public void ParryWindow_Close()  => guard?.CloseParryWindow();

    // ================= ?쇳럺???뚰뵾 李?=================
    // 臾댁씤???대┰ ?대깽???몄옄 誘몄쟾???? ??湲곕낯媛믪쑝濡??닿린/?꾩뒪
    public void PerfectDodgeWindow_Pulse()               => perfectDodge?.PerfectDodgeWindow_Pulse(DefaultPerfectDodgeWindow);
    public void PerfectDodgeWindow_Open()                => perfectDodge?.PerfectDodgeWindow_Open(DefaultPerfectDodgeWindow);
    public void PerfectDodgeWindow_Close()               => perfectDodge?.PerfectDodgeWindow_Close();

    // ?몄옄 踰꾩쟾(?대┰ ?대깽?몄뿉??珥??⑥쐞 duration ?꾨떖)
    public void PerfectDodgeWindow_Pulse(float seconds)  => perfectDodge?.PerfectDodgeWindow_Pulse(seconds);
    public void PerfectDodgeWindow_Open(float seconds)   => perfectDodge?.PerfectDodgeWindow_Open(seconds);

    // ===================== 怨듦꺽 ?뚮옒洹?=================
    // 怨듦꺽 紐⑥뀡 ?쒖옉/醫낅즺 ???좊땲硫붿씠??Bool???덉쟾?섍쾶 ?좉?
    public void AE_AttackStart()
    {
        if (anim != null && _hasAttackingBool) anim.SetBool(_attackingHash, true);
    }
    public void AE_AttackEnd()
    {
        if (anim != null && _hasAttackingBool) anim.SetBool(_attackingHash, false);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: AnimationLockManager.cs
// ==========================================

using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

public class AnimationLockManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    public Animator animator;
    Coroutine[] blendCoroutines = new Coroutine[8];
    bool isLocked = false;

    void Awake()
    {
        if (animator == null) animator = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>();
    }

    // 遺?쒕읇寃??덉씠??媛以묒튂 ?붿껌
    public void RequestLayerWeight(int layerIndex, float targetWeight, float blendTime)
    {
        if (blendCoroutines[layerIndex] != null) StopCoroutine(blendCoroutines[layerIndex]);
        blendCoroutines[layerIndex] = StartCoroutine(BlendLayerWeight(layerIndex, targetWeight, blendTime));
    }

    IEnumerator BlendLayerWeight(int layerIndex, float target, float t)
    {
        float start = animator.GetLayerWeight(layerIndex);
        float elapsed = 0f;
        while (elapsed < t)
        {
            elapsed += Time.deltaTime;
            float w = Mathf.Lerp(start, target, Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / t));
            animator.SetLayerWeight(layerIndex, w);
            yield return null;
        }
        animator.SetLayerWeight(layerIndex, target);
        blendCoroutines[layerIndex] = null;
    }

    // 利됱떆 媛뺤젣 ?ㅼ젙(湲닿툒)
    public void ForceSetLayerWeight(int layerIndex, float weight)
    {
        if (blendCoroutines[layerIndex] != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(blendCoroutines[layerIndex]);
            blendCoroutines[layerIndex] = null;
        }
        animator.SetLayerWeight(layerIndex, weight);
    }

    // ?붿껌諛쏆? ?쒓컙?숈븞 ?낅젰/?좊땲 ?쒖뼱 ?좉툑 ?뚮옒洹?(?ㅻⅨ ?쒖뒪?쒖뿉??泥댄겕 媛??
    public void RequestLockDuringSeconds(float seconds)
    {
        if (!isLocked) StartCoroutine(LockCoroutine(seconds));
    }

    IEnumerator LockCoroutine(float seconds)
    {
        isLocked = true;
        // ?꾩슂???몃? API ?몄텧 ?ъ씤?? PlayerMovement.SetExternalControl(true) ??媛??
        yield return new WaitForSeconds(seconds);
        isLocked = false;
    }

    public bool IsLocked() => isLocked;
}

// ==========================================
// File: PlayerLockManager.cs
// ==========================================

// PlayerLockManager.cs
// ?꾩뿭 ?낅젰/?대룞 ??愿由? 媛꾨떒 static API.
public static class PlayerLockManager
{
    static int _lockCount = 0;
    public static bool IsLocked => _lockCount > 0;

    public static void Lock()
    {
        _lockCount++;
    }

    public static void Unlock()
    {
        if (_lockCount > 0) _lockCount--;
    }

    public static System.IDisposable LockScope()
    {
        Lock();
        return new ScopeRelease();
    }

    class ScopeRelease : System.IDisposable
    {
        bool _disposed = false;
        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (_disposed) return;
            _disposed = true;
            Unlock();
        }
    }
}

// ?뚯씪紐? BossAnimationEvents.cs
using UnityEngine;

[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class BossAnimationEvents : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("?꾩닔 李몄“")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 FSM 而⑦듃濡ㅻ윭")]
    [SerializeField] private BossController bossController;

    [Header("臾닿린 ?덊듃諛뺤뒪 (?먮룞/?섎룞)")]
    [Tooltip("鍮꾩썙?먮㈃ ?먯떇?먯꽌 AttackHitbox ?꾨? ?먮룞 寃??)]
    [SerializeField] private AttackHitbox[] hitboxes;

    [Header("?ㅼ젙 / ?붾쾭洹?)]
    [SerializeField] private bool autoFindOnAwake = true;
    [SerializeField] private bool debugLog = false;

    void Reset()
    {
        if (!bossController)
            bossController = GetComponentInParent<BossController>();

        hitboxes = GetComponentsInChildren<AttackHitbox>(true);
    }

    void Awake()
    {
        if (autoFindOnAwake)
        {
            if (!bossController)
                bossController = GetComponentInParent<BossController>();

            if (hitboxes == null || hitboxes.Length == 0)
                hitboxes = GetComponentsInChildren<AttackHitbox>(true);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    //  ?좊땲硫붿씠???대깽?몄뿉??吏곸젒 ?몄텧???⑥닔??    //  ?대쫫??Anim_XXX 濡??댁꽌 ?ㅻⅨ 而댄룷?뚰듃? ?덈? ??寃뱀튂寃?留뚮뱺??
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /// <summary>
    /// ?덊듃諛뺤뒪 ON (怨듦꺽 ?먯젙 ?쒖옉 ?꾨젅??
    /// Animation Event: BossAnimationEvents.Anim_ActivateHitbox()
    /// </summary>
    public void Anim_ActivateHitbox()
    {
        if (debugLog) Debug.Log("[BossAnimEvents] Anim_ActivateHitbox", this);

        if (hitboxes != null && hitboxes.Length > 0)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < hitboxes.Length; i++)
            {
                if (hitboxes[i] != null)
                    hitboxes[i].ActivateWindow();
            }
        }
        else if (bossController != null)
        {
            // ?뱀떆 猷⑦듃??肄쒕씪?대뜑濡??뚮━??援ъ“瑜??????鍮꾪븳 諛깆뾽 寃쎈줈
            bossController.ActivateHitbox();
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?덊듃諛뺤뒪 OFF (怨듦꺽 ?먯젙 ???꾨젅??
    /// Animation Event: BossAnimationEvents.Anim_DeactivateHitbox()
    /// </summary>
    public void Anim_DeactivateHitbox()
    {
        if (debugLog) Debug.Log("[BossAnimEvents] Anim_DeactivateHitbox", this);

        if (hitboxes != null && hitboxes.Length > 0)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < hitboxes.Length; i++)
            {
                if (hitboxes[i] != null)
                    hitboxes[i].DeactivateWindow();
            }
        }
        else if (bossController != null)
        {
            bossController.DeactivateHitbox();
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩 ?대┰???꾩쟾???앸궗????    /// Animation Event: BossAnimationEvents.Anim_SignalPatternEnd()
    /// </summary>
    public void Anim_SignalPatternEnd()
    {
        if (debugLog) Debug.Log("[BossAnimEvents] Anim_SignalPatternEnd", this);

        if (bossController != null)
            bossController.OnAnimationPatternEnd();
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: BossBreakController.cs
// ==========================================

// Assets/Scripts/Boss/BossBreakController.cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;

[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class BossBreakController : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("01. 釉뚮젅?댄겕 寃뚯씠吏 ?ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 理쒕?移?寃뚯씠吏 1.0???대떦)")]
    [Min(1f)] public float maxBreak = 100f;

    [Tooltip("??踰??쇨꺽??湲곕낯?쇰줈 ?볦씠??釉뚮젅?댄겕??)]
    [Min(0f)] public float baseBreakPerHit = 10f;

    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 ?좎? ?쒓컙(珥?")]
    [Min(0f)] public float breakDuration = 5f;

    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭媛 ?꾨땺 ??珥덈떦 ?뚮났??)]
    [Min(0f)] public float recoveryPerSecond = 15f;

    [Header("02. 李몄“")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 泥대젰/?ㅽ깭嫄?愿由??ㅽ겕由쏀듃")]
    [SerializeField] private BossHealth bossHealth;

    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 ?좊땲硫붿씠??(IsBreak bool ?ъ슜)")]
    [SerializeField] private Animator bossAnimator;

    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭瑜??섑??대뒗 Animator Bool ?뚮씪誘명꽣 ?대쫫")]
    [SerializeField] private string breakBoolName = "IsBreak";

    [Header("03. ?대깽??(?몃? UI/?곗텧??")]
    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭??吏꾩엯?덉쓣 ???몄텧?섎뒗 ?대깽??)]
    public UnityEvent OnBreakEnter = new UnityEvent();

    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭?먯꽌 鍮좎졇?섍컝 ???몄텧?섎뒗 ?대깽??)]
    public UnityEvent OnBreakExit = new UnityEvent();

    [Header("04. ?붾쾭洹?)]
    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 愿??濡쒓렇 異쒕젰 ?щ?")]
    public bool logDebug = false;

    // ?대? ?곹깭媛?---------------------------------------------

    // ?꾩옱 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ??(0 ~ maxBreak)
    private float _currentBreak;

    // ?꾩옱 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 ?좎? ??대㉧
    private float _breakTimer;

    // 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 ?щ?
    private bool _isInBreak;

    // Animator Bool ?댁떆
    private int _breakBoolHash;

    /// <summary>
    /// 0~1濡??뺢퇋?붾맂 釉뚮젅?댄겕 寃뚯씠吏 媛?(UI??
    /// </summary>
    public float NormalizedBreak => Mathf.Clamp01(maxBreak > 0f ? _currentBreak / maxBreak : 0f);

    /// <summary>
    /// UI?먯꽌 湲곗〈???곕뜕 Get01() ?쒓렇?덉쿂 ?좎???
    /// </summary>
    public float Get01() => NormalizedBreak;

    /// <summary>
    /// ?꾩옱 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭?몄? ?щ? (蹂댁뒪 FSM ?깆뿉??議고쉶??
    /// </summary>
    public bool IsInBreak => _isInBreak;

    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    private void Reset()
    {
        if (!bossHealth) bossHealth = GetComponent<BossHealth>();
        if (!bossAnimator) bossAnimator = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>();
    }

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (!bossHealth) bossHealth = GetComponent<BossHealth>();
        if (!bossAnimator) bossAnimator = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>();

        if (bossHealth != null)
        {
            // BossHealth??OnDamaged, OnDied ?대깽?몄뿉 ?곌껐
            bossHealth.OnDamaged += HandleBossDamaged;
            bossHealth.OnDied += HandleBossDied;
        }

        _breakBoolHash = Animator.StringToHash(breakBoolName);
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (bossHealth != null)
        {
            bossHealth.OnDamaged -= HandleBossDamaged;
            bossHealth.OnDied -= HandleBossDied;
        }
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (_isInBreak)
        {
            // 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 ?좎? ?쒓컙 媛먯냼
            _breakTimer -= Time.deltaTime;
            if (_breakTimer <= 0f)
            {
                ForceExitBreak();
            }
        }
        else
        {
            // 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭媛 ?꾨땺 ???쒖꽌???뚮났
            if (_currentBreak > 0f && recoveryPerSecond > 0f)
            {
                _currentBreak = Mathf.Max(0f, _currentBreak - recoveryPerSecond * Time.deltaTime);
            }
        }
    }

    // BossHealth.OnDamaged ???곌껐?섎뒗 肄쒕갚 -------------------
    private void HandleBossDamaged(int damage)
    {
        if (damage <= 0) return;
        if (_isInBreak) return; // 釉뚮젅?댄겕以묒뿉??異붽? 異뺤쟻 ????(?꾩슂?섎㈃ ?듭뀡?쇰줈 遺꾨━ 媛??

        float add = baseBreakPerHit;
        _currentBreak = Mathf.Min(maxBreak, _currentBreak + add);

        if (logDebug)
        {
            Debug.Log(
                $"[Break] Damage={damage}, +{add} ??{_currentBreak}/{maxBreak} ({NormalizedBreak:0.00})",
                this
            );
        }

        // 寃뚯씠吏媛 理쒕????꾨떖?섎㈃ 釉뚮젅?댄겕 吏꾩엯
        if (_currentBreak >= maxBreak)
        {
            ForceEnterBreak();
        }
    }

    // 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 媛뺤젣 吏꾩엯 ---------------------------------
    public void ForceEnterBreak()
    {
        if (_isInBreak) return;

        _isInBreak = true;
        _breakTimer = breakDuration;

        // ?ㅽ깭嫄?On
        if (bossHealth != null)
        {
            // BossHealth.SetStaggered(bool) ??public ?댁뼱????
            bossHealth.SetStaggered(true);
        }

        // ?좊땲硫붿씠??bool On
        if (bossAnimator != null && _breakBoolHash != 0)
        {
            bossAnimator.SetBool(_breakBoolHash, true);
        }

        // ?대깽???몄텧 (HUD, 移대찓???곗텧 ??
        OnBreakEnter?.Invoke();

        if (logDebug)
        {
            Debug.Log("[Break] ENTER", this);
        }
    }

    // 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 媛뺤젣 醫낅즺 ---------------------------------
    public void ForceExitBreak()
    {
        if (!_isInBreak) return;

        _isInBreak = false;
        _breakTimer = 0f;

        // ?ㅽ깭嫄?Off
        if (bossHealth != null)
        {
            bossHealth.SetStaggered(false);
        }

        // ?좊땲硫붿씠??bool Off
        if (bossAnimator != null && _breakBoolHash != 0)
        {
            bossAnimator.SetBool(_breakBoolHash, false);
        }

        // 寃뚯씠吏 ?대━??
        _currentBreak = 0f;

        // ?대깽???몄텧
        OnBreakExit?.Invoke();

        if (logDebug)
        {
            Debug.Log("[Break] EXIT", this);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 寃뚯씠吏留?0?쇰줈 由ъ뀑 (?곹깭???좎?)
    /// </summary>
    public void ResetGauge()
    {
        _currentBreak = 0f;

        if (logDebug)
        {
            Debug.Log("[Break] ResetGauge()", this);
        }
    }

    // 蹂댁뒪媛 二쎌뿀????泥섎━ -----------------------------------
    private void HandleBossDied()
    {
        // 二쎌쑝硫?釉뚮젅?댄겕 醫낅즺 + 寃뚯씠吏 由ъ뀑
        ForceExitBreak();
        ResetGauge();

        // ???댁긽 ?대깽??諛쏆쓣 ?꾩슂 ?놁쓬
        if (bossHealth != null)
        {
            bossHealth.OnDamaged -= HandleBossDamaged;
            bossHealth.OnDied -= HandleBossDied;
        }
    }

    private void OnValidate()
    {
        maxBreak = Mathf.Max(1f, maxBreak);
        baseBreakPerHit = Mathf.Max(0f, baseBreakPerHit);
        breakDuration = Mathf.Max(0f, breakDuration);
        recoveryPerSecond = Mathf.Max(0f, recoveryPerSecond);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: BossController.cs
// ==========================================

// ?뚯씪紐? BossController.cs
// 蹂댁뒪 FSM + ?대룞 + ?⑦꽩 + 洹쇱젒 諛깆뒪??+ ?щ쭩 ??猷⑦듃 ?뚭눼
// ?몃━嫄곕뒗 PlayAnimTrigger() ?섎굹濡?愿由ы빐????踰덉뿉 ?섎굹留?耳쒖??꾨줉 ?ㅺ퀎.
// 怨듦꺽 吏꾩엯 ???뚮젅?댁뼱 履쎌쑝濡??뚯쟾 蹂댁젙(?ㅻ깄/吏㏃? ?뚯쟾) 吏??

using UnityEngine;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System;

public enum BossState
{
    IntroIdle,   // ?꾪닾 ?쒖옉 ???곗텧???湲?
    Detect,      // ?뚮젅?댁뼱 嫄곕━/?곹깭 泥댄겕
    Move,        // ?뚮젅?댁뼱?먭쾶 ?대룞
    Attack,      // ?⑦꽩 ?섑뻾 以?
    CombatIdle,  // 怨듦꺽 ???좉퉸 ?댁?
    Break,       // 釉뚮젅?댄겕(洹몃줈湲?
    Dead         // ?щ쭩
}

[System.Serializable]
public class AttackPattern
{
    [Header("湲곕낯 ?뺣낫 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?⑦꽩 ?대쫫(?붾쾭洹?湲덉? ?⑦꽩 議곌굔???ъ슜)")]
    public string patternName;

    [Tooltip("?좊땲硫붿씠???몃━嫄??ㅽ뀒?댄듃 ?대쫫 (?? Attack_A, Attack_B ??")]
    public string animTriggerName;

    [Tooltip("??怨듦꺽???⑤쭅 媛?ν븳 怨듦꺽?몄? ?щ?")]
    public bool isParryable;

    [Tooltip("???⑦꽩??湲곕낯 ?곕?吏??)]
    public int damageAmount = 10;

    [Header("荑⑦???/ 媛以묒튂 / 嫄곕━ 議곌굔 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?⑦꽩 ?ъ슜 ???ㅼ떆 ?ъ슜???뚭퉴吏??荑⑦???珥?")]
    public float cooldown = 2f;

    [HideInInspector] public float currentCooldown;

    [Tooltip("?⑦꽩 ?좏깮 ???쒕뜡 媛以묒튂 (媛믪씠 ?댁닔濡??좏깮???뺣쪧??")]
    public float weight = 1f;

    [Tooltip("?뚮젅?댁뼱???理쒖냼 嫄곕━ 議곌굔 (?대낫??媛源뚯슦硫??ъ슜 ????")]
    public float minRange = 0f;

    [Tooltip("?뚮젅?댁뼱???理쒕? 嫄곕━ 議곌굔 (?대낫??硫硫??ъ슜 ????")]
    public float maxRange = 5f;

    [Tooltip("???⑦꽩 吏곸쟾??湲덉????⑦꽩 ?대쫫 (?놁쑝硫?鍮?臾몄옄??")]
    public string forbiddenAfter;

    public bool CanExecute(BossController boss, float distance, string lastPattern)
    {
        if (currentCooldown > 0f) return false;
        if (distance < minRange || distance > maxRange) return false;

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(forbiddenAfter) &&
            lastPattern.Equals(forbiddenAfter, StringComparison.Ordinal))
            return false;

        if (boss.playerTracker == null) return true;

        // ?꾩슂?섎㈃ ?대쫫 湲곗??쇰줈 ?몃? 議곌굔 異붽? 媛??
        return true;
    }
}

public class BossController : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("李몄“ 而댄룷?뚰듃 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 泥대젰/?щ쭩 ?대깽???대떦 而댄룷?뚰듃")]
    public BossHealth bossHealth;

    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕(洹몃줈湲? ?곹깭 愿由?而댄룷?뚰듃")]
    public BossBreakController breakController;

    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 ?좊땲硫붿씠??(Move/Break/Dead/Attack ?뚮씪誘명꽣 ?꾨떖??")]
    public Animator bossAnimator;

    [Tooltip("?뚮젅?댁뼱 ?꾩튂 ?몃옒?뱀슜 Transform")]
    public Transform playerTarget;

    [Tooltip("怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩 鍮꾩＜??媛??媛??遺덇? ?쒖떆 ??")]
    public PatternVisuals patternVisuals;

    [Header("臾쇰━ ?대룞 ?ㅼ젙 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 ?대룞???ъ슜??Rigidbody (?놁쑝硫?Transform 吏곸젒 ?대룞)")]
    [SerializeField] private Rigidbody rb;

    [Header("怨듦꺽 ?덊듃諛뺤뒪 (怨듯넻 而댄룷?뚰듃)")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 臾닿린/???깆뿉 遺숈? AttackHitbox")]
    public AttackHitbox attackHitbox;

    [Tooltip("?뚮젅?댁뼱 ?됰룞 湲곕컲 ?⑦꽩 ?쒖뼱???몃옒而??좏깮)")]
    public PlayerTracker playerTracker;

    [Header("FSM 湲곕낯/??대컢 ?ㅼ젙 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?꾩옱 蹂댁뒪 FSM ?곹깭 (?붾쾭洹??뺤씤??")]
    public BossState currentState = BossState.IntroIdle;

    [Tooltip("?명듃濡??곗텧???湲??쒓컙 (珥?. 0?대㈃ 諛붾줈 ?꾪닾 ?쒖옉.")]
    public float introIdleDuration = 0.5f;

    [Tooltip("怨듦꺽 ??CombatIdle ?곹깭 ?좎? ?쒓컙(珥?. 0?대㈃ 諛붾줈 ?ㅼ쓬 ?곹깭濡??대룞.")]
    public float combatIdleTime = 0.3f;

    [Tooltip("怨듦꺽 ?곹깭?먯꽌 ?좊땲硫붿씠??醫낅즺瑜?湲곕떎由щ뒗 理쒕? ?쒓컙 (珥?. ?덉쟾?μ튂 ??븷")]
    public float maxAttackStateWaitTime = 2.0f;

    private Coroutine _stateRoutine;
    private bool _isDead;

    [Header("?대룞 ?ㅼ젙 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?ㅼ젣 蹂댁뒪 ?대룞 ?띾룄 (m/s)")]
    public float moveSpeed = 4.0f;

    [Tooltip("??嫄곕━ ?대궡濡??ㅼ뼱?ㅻ㈃ ?대룞??硫덉텛怨?怨듦꺽 以鍮?)]
    public float stoppingDistance = 1.0f;

    [Tooltip("Blend Tree濡??꾨떖??MoveSpeed 蹂닿컙 ?쒓컙 (0??媛源뚯슱?섎줉 利됯컖 諛섏쓳)")]
    [Range(0.01f, 0.5f)]
    public float moveAnimDamp = 0.1f;

    private static readonly int AnimParam_MoveSpeed = Animator.StringToHash("MoveSpeed");
    private static readonly int AnimParam_IsBreak   = Animator.StringToHash("IsBreak");
    private static readonly int AnimParam_IsDead    = Animator.StringToHash("IsDead");

    private float _moveBlend; // 0~1

    [Header("怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩 紐⑸줉 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪媛 ?ъ슜?????덈뒗 紐⑤뱺 怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩 由ъ뒪??)]
    public List<AttackPattern> allPatterns;

    private string _lastExecutedPattern = string.Empty;
    private AttackPattern _currentPattern;

    [Header("怨듦꺽 ???뚯쟾 蹂댁젙 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("Attack/諛깆뒪???쒖옉 ?꾩뿉 ?뚮젅?댁뼱 諛⑺뼢?쇰줈 ?뚯쟾 蹂댁젙???좎? ?щ?")]
    public bool snapRotationToPlayerOnAttack = true;

    [Tooltip("怨듦꺽 ???뚯쟾 蹂댁젙 ?쒓컙(珥?. 0?대㈃ ???꾨젅???덉뿉 諛붾줈 ?ㅻ깄 ?뚯쟾")]
    public float preAttackRotateTime = 0.0f;

    [Tooltip("怨듦꺽 ???뚯쟾 蹂댁젙???ъ슜???뚯쟾 ?띾룄(??珥?. preAttackRotateTime > 0?????ъ슜")]
    public float preAttackRotateSpeed = 720f;

    [Header("洹쇱젒 ?뚰뵾(諛깆뒪?? ?ㅼ젙 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?뚮젅?댁뼱? ?덈Т 媛源뚯슱 ??諛깆뒪???좊땲硫붿씠?섏쓣 ?ъ슜?좎? ?щ?")]
    public bool useBackstepWhenTooClose = true;

    [Tooltip("??嫄곕━蹂대떎 媛源뚯썙吏硫?諛깆뒪?앹쓣 ?쒕룄 (m)")]
    public float backstepTriggerDistance = 1.0f;

    [Tooltip("諛깆뒪???좊땲硫붿씠???몃━嫄??대쫫 (Animator ?몃━嫄??뚮씪誘명꽣? ?숈씪?섍쾶 ?ㅼ젙)")]
    public string backstepAnimTriggerName = "Backstep";

    [Tooltip("諛깆뒪??以??ㅻ줈 鍮좎????쒓컙(珥?. 猷⑦듃 紐⑥뀡 ?ъ슜 ??0?쇰줈 ?먭퀬 ?좊땲硫붿씠?섎쭔 ?ъ깮 媛??)]
    public float backstepDuration = 0.6f;

    [Tooltip("諛깆뒪??以??ㅻ줈 鍮좎????띾룄(m/s). 猷⑦듃 紐⑥뀡???대떎硫?0?쇰줈 ?먮뒗 寃껋쓣 沅뚯옣")]
    public float backstepSpeed = 4.5f;

    [Tooltip("諛깆뒪???ъ궗??荑⑦???珥?. ?덈Т ?먯＜ ?곗? ?딅룄濡??쒗븳")]
    public float backstepCooldown = 3.0f;

    private float _backstepCooldownTimer;

    [Header("?щ쭩???ㅻ툕?앺듃 ?뺣━ (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 ?щ쭩 ???먮룞?쇰줈 ?ㅻ툕?앺듃瑜??뚭눼?좎? ?щ?")]
    public bool autoDestroyOnDead = true;

    [Tooltip("?щ쭩 ?좊땲硫붿씠???곗텧 ???ㅼ젣 ??젣源뚯? ?湲??쒓컙(珥?")]
    public float destroyDelayAfterDead = 1.0f;

    [Tooltip("鍮꾩썙?먮㈃ transform.root瑜?湲곗??쇰줈 ?뚭눼, 吏?뺥븯硫??대떦 Transform 湲곗??쇰줈 ?꾩껜 ?쒓굅")]
    public Transform rootToDestroyOnDead;

    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 ?щ쭩 ???④퍡 ?뚭눼??異붽? ?ㅻ툕?앺듃??(HP諛? ?쎌삩 ?쇰쿁, ?ъ슫???ㅻ툕?앺듃 ??")]
    public Transform[] extraObjectsToDestroyOnDead;

    private Coroutine _destroyRoutine;

    void Awake()
    {
        if (attackHitbox != null)
            attackHitbox.DeactivateWindow();

        if (bossHealth != null)
            bossHealth.OnDied += OnBossDied;

        if (breakController != null)
        {
            breakController.OnBreakEnter.AddListener(OnBreakEnter);
            breakController.OnBreakExit.AddListener(OnBreakExit);
        }
    }

    void Start()
    {
        SetState(BossState.IntroIdle);
    }

    void Update()
    {
        // ?⑦꽩 荑⑦???媛먯냼
        if (allPatterns != null)
        {
            float dt = Time.deltaTime;
            foreach (var p in allPatterns)
            {
                if (p.currentCooldown > 0f)
                    p.currentCooldown -= dt;
            }
        }

        // 諛깆뒪??荑⑦???媛먯냼
        if (_backstepCooldownTimer > 0f)
            _backstepCooldownTimer -= Time.deltaTime;
    }

    // ==================== FSM ?꾩씠 ====================

    public void SetState(BossState newState)
    {
        if (_isDead) return;
        if (currentState == newState) return;

        if (_stateRoutine != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(_stateRoutine);
            _stateRoutine = null;
        }

        var old = currentState;
        currentState = newState;
        Debug.Log($"[BossFSM] {old} ??{newState}");

        switch (currentState)
        {
            case BossState.IntroIdle:
                _stateRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_HandleIntroIdle());
                break;
            case BossState.Detect:
                _stateRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_HandleDetect());
                break;
            case BossState.Move:
                _stateRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_HandleMove());
                break;
            case BossState.Attack:
                _stateRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_PerformAttack());
                break;
            case BossState.CombatIdle:
                _stateRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_CombatIdle());
                break;
            case BossState.Break:
                _stateRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_HandleBreak());
                break;
            case BossState.Dead:
                // Dead ?곹깭?먯꽌??蹂꾨룄 肄붾（???놁쓬
                break;
        }
    }

    // ==================== ?앹〈/釉뚮젅?댄겕 ====================

    void OnBossDied()
    {
        if (_isDead) return;
        _isDead = true;

        if (_stateRoutine != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(_stateRoutine);
            _stateRoutine = null;
        }

        currentState = BossState.Dead;
        UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);

        if (bossAnimator != null)
            bossAnimator.SetBool(AnimParam_IsDead, true);

        if (attackHitbox != null)
            attackHitbox.DeactivateWindow();

        Debug.Log("[BossFSM] Boss Dead");

        if (autoDestroyOnDead && _destroyRoutine == null)
            _destroyRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_DestroyHierarchyAfterDead());
    }

    void OnBreakEnter()
    {
        if (_isDead) return;

        if (bossAnimator != null)
            bossAnimator.SetBool(AnimParam_IsBreak, true);

        SetState(BossState.Break);
    }

    void OnBreakExit()
    {
        if (_isDead) return;

        if (bossAnimator != null)
            bossAnimator.SetBool(AnimParam_IsBreak, false);

        SetState(BossState.Detect);
    }

    // ==================== ?щ쭩 ???뚭눼 泥섎━ ====================

    IEnumerator Co_DestroyHierarchyAfterDead()
    {
        if (destroyDelayAfterDead > 0f)
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(destroyDelayAfterDead);

        FinalizeDeathAndDestroy();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Dead ?좊땲 ?앹뿉??AnimationEvent濡?吏곸젒 ?몄텧?섍퀬 ?띠쓣 ???ъ슜 媛??
    /// </summary>
    public void FinalizeDeathAndDestroy()
    {
        if (!_isDead)
            return;

        if (extraObjectsToDestroyOnDead != null)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < extraObjectsToDestroyOnDead.Length; ++i)
            {
                Transform t = extraObjectsToDestroyOnDead[i];
                if (t != null)
                    Destroy(t.gameObject);
            }
        }

        Transform root = rootToDestroyOnDead != null ? rootToDestroyOnDead : transform.root;
        if (root != null)
            Destroy(root.gameObject);
        else
            Destroy(gameObject);
    }

    // ==================== ?좊땲硫붿씠???대깽?몄슜 ====================

    public void OnAnimationPatternEnd()
    {
        if (currentState != BossState.Attack)
            return;

        if (_stateRoutine != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(_stateRoutine);
            _stateRoutine = null;
        }

        _currentPattern = null;
        SetState(BossState.CombatIdle);
    }

    public void ActivateHitbox()
    {
        if (_currentPattern != null && attackHitbox != null)
            attackHitbox.ActivateWindow();
    }

    public void DeactivateHitbox()
    {
        if (attackHitbox != null)
            attackHitbox.DeactivateWindow();
    }

    // ==================== ?몃━嫄??듯빀 ?좏떥 ====================

    /// <summary>
    /// 紐⑤뱺 怨듦꺽/諛깆뒪???몃━嫄곕? Reset???? ?대쾲?????몃━嫄??섎굹留?Set.
    /// ?몃━嫄곌? 寃뱀튂硫댁꽌 ?좊땲 ?곹깭 瑗ъ씠??臾몄젣瑜??덈갑?섍린 ?꾪븳 ?좏떥.
    /// </summary>
    void PlayAnimTrigger(string triggerName)
    {
        if (bossAnimator == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(triggerName))
            return;

        // 1) 怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩 ?몃━嫄??꾨? Reset
        if (allPatterns != null)
        {
            foreach (var p in allPatterns)
            {
                if (p != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(p.animTriggerName))
                    bossAnimator.ResetTrigger(p.animTriggerName);
            }
        }

        // 2) 諛깆뒪???몃━嫄?Reset
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(backstepAnimTriggerName))
            bossAnimator.ResetTrigger(backstepAnimTriggerName);

        // 3) ?대쾲?????몃━嫄곕쭔 Set
        bossAnimator.SetTrigger(triggerName);
    }

    // ==================== ?뚯쟾 蹂댁젙 ?좏떥 ====================

    /// <summary>
    /// ?뚮젅?댁뼱 諛⑺뼢?쇰줈 利됱떆 ?ㅻ깄 ?뚯쟾 (Y異뺣쭔).
    /// </summary>
    void FaceToPlayerInstant()
    {
        if (playerTarget == null) return;

        Vector3 to = playerTarget.position - transform.position;
        to.y = 0f;
        if (to.sqrMagnitude < 0.0001f) return;

        transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(to.normalized);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?쇱젙 ?쒓컙 ?숈븞 ?뚮젅?댁뼱 諛⑺뼢?쇰줈 遺?쒕읇寃??뚯쟾 (留덉?留됱뿉 ??踰????ㅻ깄).
    /// </summary>
    IEnumerator Co_FacePlayerShort(float duration, float speedDegPerSec)
    {
        if (playerTarget == null || duration <= 0f)
        {
            FaceToPlayerInstant();
            yield break;
        }

        float elapsed = 0f;
        while (elapsed < duration && !_isDead)
        {
            RotateTowardsPlayer(Time.deltaTime, speedDegPerSec);
            elapsed += Time.deltaTime;
            yield return null;
        }

        // 留덉?留됱뿉 ??踰????뺥솗??留욎떠二쇨린
        FaceToPlayerInstant();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 1?꾨젅???숈븞 ?뚮젅?댁뼱瑜??ν빐 ?뚯쟾 (RotateTowards).
    /// </summary>
    void RotateTowardsPlayer(float deltaTime, float speedDegPerSec)
    {
        if (playerTarget == null) return;

        Vector3 to = playerTarget.position - transform.position;
        to.y = 0f;
        if (to.sqrMagnitude < 0.0001f) return;

        Quaternion targetRot = Quaternion.LookRotation(to.normalized);
        transform.rotation = Quaternion.RotateTowards(
            transform.rotation, targetRot, speedDegPerSec * deltaTime);
    }

    // ==================== ?곹깭蹂?肄붾（??====================

    IEnumerator Co_HandleIntroIdle()
    {
        float t = introIdleDuration;
        while (t > 0f && !_isDead)
        {
            t -= Time.deltaTime;
            UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);
            yield return null;
        }

        if (!_isDead)
            SetState(BossState.Detect);
    }

    IEnumerator Co_HandleDetect()
    {
        yield return null;

        if (playerTarget == null)
        {
            UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);
            yield break;
        }

        float distance = Vector3.Distance(transform.position, playerTarget.position);
        UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);

        if (distance > stoppingDistance + 1.0f)
            SetState(BossState.Move);
        else
            SetState(BossState.Attack);
    }

    IEnumerator Co_HandleMove()
    {
        Debug.Log("[BossFSM] Move: ?뚮젅?댁뼱?먭쾶 ?묎렐 ?쒖옉");

        while (currentState == BossState.Move && !_isDead)
        {
            if (playerTarget == null)
            {
                UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);
                yield break;
            }

            Vector3 toPlayer = playerTarget.position - transform.position;
            toPlayer.y = 0f;
            float distance = toPlayer.magnitude;

            if (distance <= stoppingDistance)
            {
                UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);
                SetState(BossState.Attack);
                yield break;
            }

            Vector3 dir = toPlayer.normalized;
            if (dir.sqrMagnitude > 0.0001f)
            {
                Quaternion look = Quaternion.LookRotation(dir);
                transform.rotation = Quaternion.Slerp(
                    transform.rotation, look, Time.deltaTime * 5f);
            }

            Vector3 delta = dir * moveSpeed * Time.deltaTime;
            if (rb != null)
                rb.MovePosition(rb.position + delta);
            else
                transform.position += delta;

            UpdateMoveAnimation(1f);
            yield return null;
        }
    }

    IEnumerator Co_CombatIdle()
    {
        float t = combatIdleTime;
        while (t > 0f && !_isDead)
        {
            t -= Time.deltaTime;
            UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);
            yield return null;
        }

        if (!_isDead)
            SetState(BossState.Detect);
    }

    IEnumerator Co_HandleBreak()
    {
        Debug.Log("[BossFSM] Break: 釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭 吏꾩엯");
        while (currentState == BossState.Break && !_isDead)
        {
            UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);
            yield return null;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 怨듦꺽 ?곹깭 吏꾩엯 ?? (?뚯쟾 蹂댁젙) ??洹쇱젒?대㈃ 諛깆뒪?? ?꾨땲硫??쇰컲 怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩 ?섑뻾
    /// </summary>
    IEnumerator Co_PerformAttack()
    {
        if (playerTarget == null)
        {
            SetState(BossState.CombatIdle);
            yield break;
        }

        UpdateMoveAnimation(0f);

        // 0) 怨듦꺽 ?쒖옉 ?꾩뿉 ?뚮젅?댁뼱 履쎌쑝濡??뚯쟾 蹂댁젙
        if (snapRotationToPlayerOnAttack)
        {
            if (preAttackRotateTime > 0f)
                yield return StartCoroutine(Co_FacePlayerShort(preAttackRotateTime, preAttackRotateSpeed));
            else
                FaceToPlayerInstant();
        }

        // 1) 嫄곕━ ?ㅼ떆 痢≪젙 ??諛깆뒪???щ? 寃곗젙
        float distance = Vector3.Distance(transform.position, playerTarget.position);

        if (useBackstepWhenTooClose &&
            distance < backstepTriggerDistance &&
            _backstepCooldownTimer <= 0f)
        {
            yield return StartCoroutine(Co_Backstep());

            _backstepCooldownTimer = backstepCooldown;

            if (!_isDead)
                SetState(BossState.CombatIdle);

            yield break;
        }

        // 2) ?쇰컲 怨듦꺽 ?⑦꽩
        if (allPatterns == null || allPatterns.Count == 0)
        {
            SetState(BossState.CombatIdle);
            yield break;
        }

        AttackPattern pattern = SelectPattern(distance);
        if (pattern == null)
        {
            Debug.LogWarning("[BossFSM] ?ъ슜?????덈뒗 ?⑦꽩???놁쓬 ??CombatIdle");
            SetState(BossState.CombatIdle);
            yield break;
        }

        _currentPattern         = pattern;
        _lastExecutedPattern    = pattern.patternName;
        pattern.currentCooldown = pattern.cooldown;

        if (patternVisuals != null)
            patternVisuals.SetParryable(pattern.isParryable);

        if (attackHitbox != null)
            attackHitbox.Configure(pattern.damageAmount, pattern.isParryable, transform);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pattern.animTriggerName))
            PlayAnimTrigger(pattern.animTriggerName);

        Debug.Log($"[BossFSM] Attack ?⑦꽩 ?ㅽ뻾: {pattern.patternName} ({pattern.animTriggerName})");

        // ?대쫫 湲곕컲 ?ㅽ뀒?댄듃 ?湲곌? ?꾨땲??
        // "Attack ?곹깭 + 理쒕? ?湲??쒓컙" 湲곗??쇰줈留?湲곕떎由щ뒗 諛⑹떇 (?덉쟾?μ튂).
        float maxWait = Mathf.Max(0.1f, maxAttackStateWaitTime);
        float elapsed = 0f;

        while (elapsed < maxWait && currentState == BossState.Attack && !_isDead)
        {
            elapsed += Time.deltaTime;
            yield return null;
        }

        if (currentState == BossState.Attack && !_isDead)
        {
            _currentPattern = null;
            SetState(BossState.CombatIdle);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?뚮젅?댁뼱? ?덈Т 媛源뚯슱 ???ㅽ뻾?섎뒗 諛깆뒪???꾩슜 肄붾（??
    /// (猷⑦듃 紐⑥뀡 諛깆뒪?앹씠硫?backstepSpeed=0?쇰줈 ?먮뒗 嫄?沅뚯옣)
    /// </summary>
    IEnumerator Co_Backstep()
    {
        if (playerTarget == null)
            yield break;

        // 諛깆뒪?앸룄 癒쇱? ?뚮젅?댁뼱瑜?諛붾씪蹂닿퀬 ?쒖옉
        FaceToPlayerInstant();

        if (attackHitbox != null)
            attackHitbox.DeactivateWindow();

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(backstepAnimTriggerName))
            PlayAnimTrigger(backstepAnimTriggerName);

        float elapsed = 0f;
        Vector3 backDir = -transform.forward;

        while (elapsed < backstepDuration && !_isDead)
        {
            if (backstepSpeed > 0f)
            {
                Vector3 delta = backDir * backstepSpeed * Time.deltaTime;
                if (rb != null)
                    rb.MovePosition(rb.position + delta);
                else
                    transform.position += delta;
            }

            elapsed += Time.deltaTime;
            yield return null;
        }
    }

    // ==================== ?⑦꽩 ?좏깮 ====================

    AttackPattern SelectPattern(float distanceToPlayer)
    {
        if (allPatterns == null || allPatterns.Count == 0)
            return null;

        List<AttackPattern> candidates = new List<AttackPattern>();

        foreach (var p in allPatterns)
        {
            if (p == null) continue;
            if (p.CanExecute(this, distanceToPlayer, _lastExecutedPattern))
                candidates.Add(p);
        }

        if (candidates.Count == 0)
            return null;

        float totalWeight = 0f;
        foreach (var p in candidates)
            totalWeight += Mathf.Max(0f, p.weight);

        if (totalWeight <= 0f)
            return candidates[0];

        float r = UnityEngine.Random.value * totalWeight;
        float accum = 0f;

        foreach (var p in candidates)
        {
            float w = Mathf.Max(0f, p.weight);
            accum += w;
            if (r <= accum)
                return p;
        }

        return candidates[candidates.Count - 1];
    }

    // ==================== ?대룞 ?좊땲 (BlendTree) ====================

    void UpdateMoveAnimation(float target01)
    {
        if (bossAnimator == null) return;

        target01 = Mathf.Clamp01(target01);

        _moveBlend = Mathf.Lerp(
            _moveBlend,
            target01,
            Time.deltaTime / Mathf.Max(0.0001f, moveAnimDamp)
        );

        bossAnimator.SetFloat(AnimParam_MoveSpeed, _moveBlend);
    }

    // ==================== (?좏깮) 猷⑦듃 肄쒕씪?대뜑 異⑸룎 ====================

    void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
    {
        if (_currentPattern == null) return;
        // ?꾩슂?섎㈃ ?ш린???뱀닔 泥섎━ 異붽?.
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: BossDamageReceiver.cs
// ==========================================

// Assets/Scripts/Boss/BossDamageReceiver.cs
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// 蹂댁뒪媛 ?뚮젅?댁뼱 怨듦꺽(臾닿린 AttackHitbox ????留욎쓣 ??
/// 怨듯넻 ?명꽣?섏씠??IDamageReceiver)瑜??듯빐 ?곕?吏瑜?諛쏅뒗 而댄룷?뚰듃
/// </summary>
[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class BossDamageReceiver : MonoBehaviour, IDamageReceiver
{
    [Header("李몄“")]
    public BossHealth bossHealth;
    public Transform bossRoot;

    [Header("?덊듃 ????ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ?뺣낫?먯꽌 HitType ??臾댁떆?섍퀬 ??媛믪쑝濡?媛뺤젣?좎? ?щ?")]
    public bool overrideHitType = false;

    [Tooltip("overrideHitType ??true ?????ъ슜??湲곕낯 ???)]
    public HitType overrideType = HitType.Normal;

    void Awake()
    {
        if (bossHealth == null)
            bossHealth = GetComponentInParent<BossHealth>();

        if (bossRoot == null && bossHealth != null)
            bossRoot = bossHealth.transform;
    }

    public void ReceiveHit(HitPayload payload)
    {
        if (bossHealth == null)
            return;

        HitType type = overrideHitType ? overrideType : payload.hitType;

        // IHealth 援ы쁽 湲곕컲 BossHealth.TakeDamage(...)
        bossHealth.TakeDamage(
            Mathf.RoundToInt(payload.damage),
            type,
            payload.hitPoint
        );
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: BossHealth.cs
// ==========================================

// Assets/Scripts/Boss/BossHealth.cs
using System;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// 蹂댁뒪??泥대젰 而댄룷?뚰듃(IHealth 援ы쁽).
/// - HP/臾댁쟻/寃쎌쭅/?щ쭩 ?대깽??愿由?
/// - BossBreakController, BossController ?깆뿉??怨듯넻?쇰줈 李몄“
/// </summary>
[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class BossHealth : MonoBehaviour, IHealth
{
    [Header("??泥대젰 ?ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 理쒕? 泥대젰")]
    [Min(1)]
    [SerializeField] int maxHP = 100;

    [Tooltip("?쒖옉 泥대젰 (0 ?댄븯?대㈃ MaxHP濡??쒖옉)")]
    [SerializeField] int startHP = 0;

    [Header("??臾댁쟻/寃쎌쭅 ?듭뀡")]
    [Tooltip("?쒖옉 ??臾댁쟻 ?곹깭濡??쒖옉?좎? ?щ?")]
    [SerializeField] bool invincibleOnStart = false;

    [Tooltip("?쇨꺽 ???먮룞 臾댁쟻 ?쒓컙(珥?. 0?대㈃ ?먮룞 臾댁쟻 ?놁쓬")]
    [Min(0f)]
    [SerializeField] float hitInvincibleDuration = 0f;

    [Tooltip("?쒖옉 ??寃쎌쭅 ?곹깭 ?щ?(?⑦꽩???곕씪 ?몃??먯꽌 蹂寃?媛??")]
    [SerializeField] bool startStaggered = false;

    [Header("???붾쾭洹??숈옉 ?듭뀡")]
    [Tooltip("?щ쭩 ??寃뚯엫?ㅻ툕?앺듃 鍮꾪솢?깊솕 ?щ?")]
    [SerializeField] bool disableOnDeath = false;

    [Tooltip("?붾쾭洹?濡쒓렇 異쒕젰 ?щ?")]
    [SerializeField] bool debugLog = false;

    // ===== IHealth ?꾨줈?쇳떚 =====
    public int MaxHP       => maxHP;
    public int CurrentHP   { get; private set; }
    public bool IsDead     { get; private set; }
    public bool IsStaggered { get; private set; }
    public bool isInvincible { get; set; }

    // ===== IHealth ?대깽??=====
    public event Action<int, int> OnHPChanged;
    public event Action<int, int> OnHealthChanged;

    public event Action<int> OnDamaged;
    public event Action<int, HitType> OnDamagedWithType;
    public event Action OnDied;

    public event Action OnStagger;

    // ?대? ?곹깭
    float _invincibleRemain;

    void Awake()
    {
        if (startHP <= 0)
            CurrentHP = maxHP;
        else
            CurrentHP = Mathf.Clamp(startHP, 0, maxHP);

        IsDead      = CurrentHP <= 0;
        IsStaggered = startStaggered;
        isInvincible = invincibleOnStart;

        if (invincibleOnStart && hitInvincibleDuration > 0f)
            _invincibleRemain = hitInvincibleDuration;

        // 珥덇린 泥대젰 釉뚮줈?쒖틦?ㅽ듃 (UI ?숆린?붿슜)
        OnHPChanged?.Invoke(CurrentHP, MaxHP);
        OnHealthChanged?.Invoke(CurrentHP, MaxHP);

        if (debugLog)
            Debug.Log($"[BossHealth] Init HP={CurrentHP}/{MaxHP} invincible={isInvincible} staggered={IsStaggered}", this);
    }

    void Update()
    {
        if (_invincibleRemain > 0f)
        {
            _invincibleRemain -= Time.deltaTime;
            if (_invincibleRemain <= 0f)
            {
                _invincibleRemain = 0f;
                isInvincible = false;

                if (debugLog)
                    Debug.Log("[BossHealth] Invincible OFF (timer end)", this);
            }
        }
    }

    // ===== IHealth 援ы쁽 =====

    /// <summary>泥대젰 ?뚮났 (?뚯닔硫?臾댁떆)</summary>
    public void Heal(int amount)
    {
        if (IsDead || amount <= 0) return;

        int prev = CurrentHP;
        CurrentHP = Mathf.Clamp(CurrentHP + amount, 0, MaxHP);
        if (CurrentHP == prev) return;

        OnHPChanged?.Invoke(CurrentHP, MaxHP);
        OnHealthChanged?.Invoke(CurrentHP, MaxHP);

        if (debugLog)
            Debug.Log($"[BossHealth] Heal {amount} => {CurrentHP}/{MaxHP}", this);
    }

    /// <summary>遺?숈냼???곕?吏???대??먯꽌 int濡?蹂????泥섎━</summary>
    public void ApplyDamage(float amount)
    {
        int dmg = Mathf.RoundToInt(amount);
        if (dmg <= 0) return;

        TakeDamage(dmg, HitType.Normal, transform.position);
    }

    /// <summary>湲곕낯 ?곕?吏 泥섎━(??? Normal)</summary>
    public void ApplyDamage(int amount)
    {
        if (amount <= 0) return;

        TakeDamage(amount, HitType.Normal, transform.position);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?덊듃????덊듃 ?꾩튂源뚯? ?ы븿???곕?吏 泥섎━.
    /// HitType.Force ??臾댁쟻??臾댁떆?섍퀬 媛뺤젣 ?곕?吏濡??ㅼ뼱媛?꾨줉 ?ㅺ퀎.
    /// </summary>
    public void TakeDamage(int damage, HitType hitType, Vector3 hitPoint)
    {
        if (damage <= 0) return;
        if (IsDead) return;

        // 媛뺤젣 ?곕?吏媛 ?꾨땶 ?댁긽 臾댁쟻?대㈃ 臾댁떆
        if (isInvincible && hitType != HitType.Force) return;

        int prev = CurrentHP;
        CurrentHP = Mathf.Clamp(CurrentHP - damage, 0, MaxHP);

        OnHPChanged?.Invoke(CurrentHP, MaxHP);
        OnHealthChanged?.Invoke(CurrentHP, MaxHP);
        OnDamaged?.Invoke(damage);
        OnDamagedWithType?.Invoke(damage, hitType);

        if (debugLog)
            Debug.Log($"[BossHealth] TakeDamage({hitType}) {damage} => {prev}->{CurrentHP}/{MaxHP}", this);

        if (CurrentHP <= 0 && !IsDead)
        {
            Die();
            return;
        }

        // ?덊듃 ???먮룞 臾댁쟻 (Force ????쒖쇅)
        if (hitInvincibleDuration > 0f && hitType != HitType.Force)
            SetInvincible(hitInvincibleDuration);
    }

    /// <summary>臾댁쟻 ?좉? + ??대㉧ 媛깆떊</summary>
    public void SetInvincible(float duration)
    {
        if (duration <= 0f)
        {
            isInvincible = false;
            _invincibleRemain = 0f;

            if (debugLog)
                Debug.Log("[BossHealth] Invincible OFF (manual)", this);

            return;
        }

        isInvincible = true;
        _invincibleRemain = duration;

        if (debugLog)
            Debug.Log($"[BossHealth] Invincible ON ({duration:0.00}s)", this);
    }

    /// <summary>寃쎌쭅 ?곹깭 ?ㅼ젙 (釉뚮젅?댄겕, ?⑦꽩 ?깆뿉???몄텧)</summary>
    // BossHealth.cs ?덉뿉 ?덈뒗 SetStaggered瑜???踰꾩쟾?쇰줈 援먯껜
    public void SetStaggered(bool stagger)
{
    if (IsStaggered == stagger)
        return;

    IsStaggered = stagger;

    if (debugLog)
        Debug.Log("[BossHealth] Stagger=" + (stagger ? "ON" : "OFF"), this);

    if (stagger)
        OnStagger?.Invoke();
}



    /// <summary>媛뺤젣濡?利됱떆 ?щ쭩 泥섎━</summary>
    public void Kill()
    {
        if (IsDead) return;

        CurrentHP = 0;
        Die();
    }

    void Die()
    {
        if (IsDead) return;

        IsDead = true;

        if (debugLog)
            Debug.Log("[BossHealth] DIE", this);

        OnDied?.Invoke();

        if (disableOnDeath)
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
    }

#if UNITY_EDITOR
    void OnValidate()
    {
        maxHP = Mathf.Max(1, maxHP);

        // ?먮뵒?곗뿉??startHP瑜?0 ?댄븯濡??먮㈃ ?먮룞?쇰줈 MaxHP濡?留욎텛湲?
        if (startHP <= 0)
            startHP = maxHP;
    }
#endif
}

// ==========================================
// File: BossHitReaction.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// 蹂댁뒪媛 ?곕?吏瑜?諛쏆쓣 ??諛쒖깮?섎뒗 鍮꾩＜???좊땲硫붿씠???ъ슫??諛섏쓳 ?꾨떞 而댄룷?뚰듃.
/// - BossHealth.OnDamagedWithType 瑜?援щ룆?댁꽌,
///   * ?덊듃 ?댄럺???ㅽ룿
///   * Hit / Stagger ?좊땲硫붿씠???몃━嫄??몄텧
///   * ?덊듃 ?ъ슫???ъ깮
/// 瑜??대떦?쒕떎.
/// </summary>
[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class BossHitReaction : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("李몄“ (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("媛숈? ?ㅻ툕?앺듃 ?먮뒗 遺紐⑥뿉 ?덈뒗 BossHealth瑜?吏?? 鍮꾩썙?먮㈃ ?먮룞 寃??")]
    public BossHealth bossHealth;
    
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 ?좊땲硫붿씠?? Hit / Stagger ?몃━嫄곕? 蹂대궪 ???")]
    public Animator bossAnimator;

    [Header("?덊듃 ?댄럺??(?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?쇰컲 怨듦꺽 ?쇨꺽 ???ъ슜??VFX ?꾨━??(?좏깮).")]
    public GameObject normalHitVfx;

    [Tooltip("媛뺢났寃?寃쎌쭅 怨듦꺽 ?쇨꺽 ???ъ슜??VFX ?꾨━??(?좏깮).")]
    public GameObject heavyHitVfx;

    [Tooltip("?댄럺???앹꽦 ?꾩튂 湲곗? ?몃옖?ㅽ뤌 (?놁쑝硫?蹂댁뒪 以묒떖 ?꾩튂 ?ъ슜).")]
    public Transform vfxPivot;

    [Header("?좊땲硫붿씠???뚮씪誘명꽣 ?대쫫 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?쇰컲 ?쇨꺽 ???ъ슜???몃━嫄??대쫫 (?? \"Hit\"). 鍮꾩썙?먮㈃ ?좊땲???몄텧?섏? ?딆쓬.")]
    public string hitTriggerName = "Hit";

    [Tooltip("寃쎌쭅/媛뺢났寃??쇨꺽 ???ъ슜???몃━嫄??대쫫 (?? \"Stagger\").")]
    public string staggerTriggerName = "Stagger";

    [Header("寃쎌쭅 議곌굔 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("??HitType???대떦??寃쎌슦 寃쎌쭅 ?좊땲硫붿씠?섍낵 ?ㅻ퉬 ?댄럺???ъ슫?쒕? ?ъ슜?쒕떎.")]
    public HitType staggerHitType = HitType.Heavy;  // ?꾨줈?앺듃??HitType ?뺤쓽??留욊쾶 媛믩쭔 留욎떠???ъ슜

    [Header("?덊듃 ?ъ슫???ㅼ젙 (?쒓? ?ㅻ챸)")]
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ?ъ슫?쒕? ?ъ깮??AudioSource (?놁쑝硫??고??꾩뿉 ???ㅻ툕?앺듃???먮룞 異붽?).")]
    public AudioSource audioSource;   // [議곗젅媛?

    [Tooltip("?쇰컲 ?쇨꺽 ???ъ깮???ъ슫???대┰??(?щ윭 媛??ｌ쑝硫??쒕뜡?쇰줈 ?섎굹 ?ъ깮).")]
    public AudioClip[] normalHitClips;    // [議곗젅媛?

    [Tooltip("寃쎌쭅/媛뺢났寃??쇨꺽 ???ъ깮???ъ슫???대┰??(?щ윭 媛??ｌ쑝硫??쒕뜡?쇰줈 ?섎굹 ?ъ깮).")]
    public AudioClip[] heavyHitClips;     // [議곗젅媛?

    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ?ъ슫??湲곕낯 蹂쇰ⅷ (0~1)")]
    [Range(0f, 1f)]
    public float hitVolume = 1f;         // [議곗젅媛?

    [Tooltip("媛숈? ?ъ슫?쒓? 諛섎났???뚯쓽 ?⑥“濡쒖???以꾩씠湲??꾪븳 ?쒕뜡 ?쇱튂 踰붿쐞 (x = 理쒖냼, y = 理쒕?). ????1?대㈃ ?쇱튂 怨좎젙.")]
    public Vector2 randomPitchRange = new Vector2(0.95f, 1.05f); // [議곗젅媛?

    void Awake()
    {
        if (!bossHealth)
            bossHealth = GetComponent<BossHealth>();

        if (!bossAnimator && bossHealth)
            bossAnimator = bossHealth.GetComponentInChildren<Animator>();

        if (!bossHealth)
        {
            Debug.LogError("[BossHitReaction] BossHealth瑜?李얠? 紐삵뻽?듬땲??", this);
            enabled = false;
            return;
        }

        // ?덊듃 ?대깽??援щ룆
        // IHealth.OnDamagedWithType: Action<int, HitType> ?뺥깭?쇨퀬 媛??
        bossHealth.OnDamagedWithType += HandleDamagedWithType;

        // ?덊듃 ?ъ슫?쒖슜 AudioSource 以鍮?(?놁쑝硫??먮룞 ?앹꽦)
        EnsureAudioSource();
    }

    void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (bossHealth != null)
            bossHealth.OnDamagedWithType -= HandleDamagedWithType;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// BossHealth?먯꽌 ?곕?吏瑜?諛쏆? 吏곹썑 ?몄텧?섎뒗 肄쒕갚.
    /// ?ш린???댄럺???좊땲/?ъ슫?쒕? 紐⑤몢 泥섎━?쒕떎.
    /// </summary>
    void HandleDamagedWithType(int damage, HitType hitType)
    {
        // 1) VFX ?ㅽ룿
        SpawnHitVfx(hitType);

        // 2) ?좊땲硫붿씠???몃━嫄?
        PlayHitAnimation(hitType);

        // 3) ?ъ슫???ъ깮
        PlayHitSound(hitType);
    }

    // ==================== VFX ====================

    void SpawnHitVfx(HitType hitType)
    {
        GameObject prefab = null;

        // 寃쎌쭅(Heavy) 怨듦꺽?몄????곕씪 ?ㅻⅨ ?댄럺???좏깮
        if (hitType == staggerHitType && heavyHitVfx != null)
            prefab = heavyHitVfx;
        else if (normalHitVfx != null)
            prefab = normalHitVfx;

        if (!prefab) return;

        Vector3 spawnPos = vfxPivot ? vfxPivot.position : transform.position;
        Instantiate(prefab, spawnPos, Quaternion.identity);
    }

    // ==================== ?좊땲硫붿씠??====================

    void PlayHitAnimation(HitType hitType)
    {
        if (!bossAnimator) return;

        // 寃쎌쭅 怨듦꺽?대㈃ Stagger ?곗꽑
        if (hitType == staggerHitType && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(staggerTriggerName))
        {
            bossAnimator.ResetTrigger(staggerTriggerName);
            bossAnimator.SetTrigger(staggerTriggerName);
            return;
        }

        // 洹??몄뿉???쇰컲 Hit
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hitTriggerName))
        {
            bossAnimator.ResetTrigger(hitTriggerName);
            bossAnimator.SetTrigger(hitTriggerName);
        }
    }

    // ==================== ?ъ슫??====================

    void EnsureAudioSource()
    {
        if (audioSource != null) return;

        // 癒쇱? ?먭린 ?ㅻ툕?앺듃?먯꽌 AudioSource 李얠븘蹂닿퀬
        audioSource = GetComponent<AudioSource>();
        if (audioSource != null) return;

        // ?놁쑝硫??덈줈 異붽? (3D ?덊듃 ?ъ슫???⑸룄)
        audioSource = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
        audioSource.playOnAwake = false;
        audioSource.spatialBlend = 1.0f;       // 3D ?ъ슫??
        audioSource.rolloffMode = AudioRolloffMode.Linear;
        audioSource.maxDistance = 30f;         // ?꾩슂 ??議곗젅
    }

    void PlayHitSound(HitType hitType)
    {
        if (audioSource == null) return;

        AudioClip clip = GetClipForHitType(hitType);
        if (clip == null) return;

        // ?쒕뜡 ?쇱튂 ?곸슜
        float pitch = 1f;
        if (randomPitchRange.y > 0f && randomPitchRange.y >= randomPitchRange.x)
        {
            pitch = Random.Range(randomPitchRange.x, randomPitchRange.y);
        }

        audioSource.pitch = pitch;

        // PlayOneShot ?ъ슜: 媛숈? ?꾨젅?꾩뿉 ?щ윭 ?덊듃媛 ?ㅼ뼱???寃뱀퀜???ъ깮 媛??
        audioSource.PlayOneShot(clip, hitVolume);
    }

    AudioClip GetClipForHitType(HitType hitType)
    {
        // 寃쎌쭅 ??낆씠硫??ㅻ퉬 ?대┰ ?곗꽑
        if (hitType == staggerHitType)
        {
            AudioClip heavy = GetRandomClip(heavyHitClips);
            if (heavy != null)
                return heavy;
        }

        // ?섎㉧吏???쇰컲 ?덊듃
        return GetRandomClip(normalHitClips);
    }

    AudioClip GetRandomClip(AudioClip[] clips)
    {
        if (clips == null || clips.Length == 0)
            return null;

        int idx = Random.Range(0, clips.Length);
        return clips[idx];
    }
}

// ?뚯씪紐? PatternVisuals.cs
using UnityEngine;
using System.Collections;

[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class PatternVisuals : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("鍮꾩＜???源??뚮뜑??)]
    [Tooltip("蹂댁뒪 紐⑤뜽 以??됱쓣 源쒕묀?닿쾶 ??Renderer. 鍮꾩썙?먮㈃ ?먯떇?먯꽌 ?먮룞?쇰줈 李얠뒿?덈떎.")]
    public Renderer visualPartRenderer;

    [Header("?먯씠??而щ윭 ?꾨줈?쇳떚 ?대쫫")]
    [Tooltip("HDR Emission 而щ윭 ?꾨줈?쇳떚 ?대쫫 (?? _EmissionColor, _BaseColor ??")]
    public string emissionColorName = "_EmissionColor";

    [Header("?됱긽 ?ㅼ젙 (HDR 沅뚯옣)")]
    [Tooltip("?⑤쭅/媛??媛?ν븳 怨듦꺽 ?됱긽")]
    [ColorUsage(true, true)]
    public Color parryColor = new Color(1f, 0.5f, 0f) * 5f;

    [Tooltip("?⑤쭅/媛??遺덇?(?멸??붾툝) 怨듦꺽 ?됱긽")]
    [ColorUsage(true, true)]
    public Color nonParryColor = Color.red * 5f;

    [Tooltip("湲곕낯/?꾩씠???곹깭 ?됱긽")]
    [ColorUsage(true, true)]
    public Color idleColor = Color.black;

    [Header("?뚮옒????대컢")]
    [Tooltip("??踰?源쒕묀?대뒗 ?꾩껜 ?쒓컙(珥?")]
    [Min(0.01f)]
    public float flashDuration = 0.5f;

    // ?대???
    Material _runtimeMaterial;
    Coroutine _flashRoutine;

    void Awake()
    {
        // ?뚮뜑???먮룞 ?먯깋 (吏?????덉쓣 ??
        if (!visualPartRenderer)
        {
            visualPartRenderer = GetComponentInChildren<Renderer>(true);
        }

        if (!visualPartRenderer)
        {
            Debug.LogError("[PatternVisuals] ?源?Renderer媛 ?ㅼ젙?섏? ?딆븯?듬땲??", this);
            enabled = false;
            return;
        }

        // ??蹂댁뒪 ?꾩슜 癒명꽣由ъ뼹 ?몄뒪?댁뒪 ?앹꽦
        // (二쇱쓽: material ?ъ슜? ?몄뒪?댁뒪瑜?留뚮뱾湲??뚮Ц?? ?고????앹꽦 媛쒖닔媛 留롮쑝硫?鍮꾩슜??而ㅼ쭏 ???덉쓬)
        _runtimeMaterial = visualPartRenderer.material;

        // ?쒖옉 ??湲곕낯 ?됱쑝濡?珥덇린??
        SetEmissionColor(idleColor);
    }

    void OnDisable()
    {
        // 而댄룷?뚰듃媛 爰쇱쭏 ??源쒕묀??以묒씠硫??뺣━ + 湲곕낯?됱쑝濡?蹂듦?
        StopFlash();
        SetEmissionColor(idleColor);
    }

    // ======================================================================
    //  ?몃??먯꽌 ?몄텧?섎뒗 API
    //  BossController ?먯꽌 patternVisuals.SetParryable(pattern.isParryable)
    //  ?먮뒗 StartVisualCue(true/false) 濡??몄텧?섎㈃ ??
    // ======================================================================

    /// <summary>
    /// BossController?먯꽌 ?⑦꽩 吏꾩엯 ???몄텧?섎뒗 吏꾩엯??
    /// ?대??곸쑝濡?StartVisualCue瑜??몄텧?쒕떎.
    /// </summary>
    public void SetParryable(bool isParryable)
    {
        StartVisualCue(isParryable);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// isParryable???곕씪 ?됱쓣 ??踰?源쒕묀?몃떎.
    /// </summary>
    public void StartVisualCue(bool isParryable)
    {
        if (!isActiveAndEnabled) return;

        if (_flashRoutine != null)
            StopCoroutine(_flashRoutine);

        _flashRoutine = StartCoroutine(Co_FlashVisuals(isParryable));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?몃??먯꽌 媛뺤젣濡?湲곕낯 ?됱쑝濡??섎룎由ш퀬 ?띠쓣 ???ъ슜.
    /// (?? 怨듦꺽 ?좊땲 ??AnimationEvent?먯꽌 ?몄텧)
    /// </summary>
    public void ResetToIdle()
    {
        StopFlash();
        SetEmissionColor(idleColor);
    }

    // ======================================================================
    //  ?대? 援ы쁽
    // ======================================================================

    IEnumerator Co_FlashVisuals(bool isParryable)
    {
        Color targetColor = isParryable ? parryColor : nonParryColor;

        // ?щ줈?곕え???곹뼢??諛쏆? ?딄쾶 unscaledDeltaTime ?ъ슜
        SetEmissionColor(targetColor);

        float t = 0f;
        while (t < flashDuration)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            yield return null;
        }

        SetEmissionColor(idleColor);
        _flashRoutine = null;
    }

    void StopFlash()
    {
        if (_flashRoutine != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(_flashRoutine);
            _flashRoutine = null;
        }
    }

    void SetEmissionColor(Color color)
    {
        if (_runtimeMaterial == null) return;
        _runtimeMaterial.SetColor(emissionColorName, color);
    }

#if UNITY_EDITOR
    // ?먮뵒?곗뿉???고겢由?쑝濡?諛붾줈 ?뚯뒪?명븷 ???덈뒗 硫붾돱
    [ContextMenu("Test Parryable Flash")]
    void TestParryableFlash()
    {
        StartVisualCue(true);
    }

    [ContextMenu("Test Non-Parryable Flash")]
    void TestNonParryableFlash()
    {
        StartVisualCue(false);
    }

    [ContextMenu("Reset To Idle Color")]
    void TestResetIdle()
    {
        ResetToIdle();
    }
#endif
}

// ==========================================
// File: PlayerTracker.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using System;

// BossController와 Pattern.CanExecute에서 PlayerTracker의 데이터를 참조하기 위한 인터페이스
public interface IPlayerTracker
{
    bool IsDodgingRecently();
    bool IsAttackStoppedRecently(float duration);
    bool IsRetreating();
    void SignalPlayerAction(PlayerAction action);
}

// 플레이어 행동 정의
public enum PlayerAction { Attack, Dodge, Idle }

// 파일명: PlayerTracker.cs
public class PlayerTracker : MonoBehaviour, IPlayerTracker
{
    [Header("추적 시간 설정")]
    public float recentTimeThreshold = 3f; // 최근 행동을 판단하는 기준 시간 (3초)
    public int dodgeCountThreshold = 2; // 연속 회피 판단 횟수 (2회)
    public float retreatDurationThreshold = 2f; // 후퇴 지속 시간 (2초)

    private float _lastAttackTime = -10f;
    private float _lastDodgeTime = -10f;
    private int _recentDodgeCount = 0;
    private float _retreatStartTime = -10f;

    // --- IPlayerTracker 구현 ---

    // 사선 베기 조건: 플레이어가 최근 3초 내 회피를 2회 이상 연속 사용했는지
    public bool IsDodgingRecently()
    {
        return (Time.time - _lastDodgeTime <= recentTimeThreshold) && (_recentDodgeCount >= dodgeCountThreshold);
    }

    // 연속 베기 조건: 플레이어가 일정 시간 공격을 멈췄는지
    public bool IsAttackStoppedRecently(float duration)
    {
        return (Time.time - _lastAttackTime >= duration);
    }

    // 대쉬 찌르기 조건: 플레이어가 반복적으로 후퇴할 때 (2m 이상 거리에서 2초 이상)
    public bool IsRetreating()
    {
        return (Time.time - _retreatStartTime >= retreatDurationThreshold);
    }

    // (참고: 도약 내려찍기의 '회복 캡슐 사용' 조건은 외부 이벤트 시스템 연동 필요)

    public void SignalPlayerAction(PlayerAction action)
    {
        float currentTime = Time.time;

        switch (action)
        {
            case PlayerAction.Attack:
                _lastAttackTime = currentTime;
                _retreatStartTime = -10f;
                break;
            case PlayerAction.Dodge:
                if (currentTime - _lastDodgeTime <= 1.0f) // 짧은 시간 내 연속 회피 체크
                {
                    _recentDodgeCount++;
                }
                else
                {
                    _recentDodgeCount = 1;
                }
                _lastDodgeTime = currentTime;
                _retreatStartTime = -10f;
                break;
            case PlayerAction.Idle:
                // 플레이어가 후퇴(뒤로 이동)하기 시작했을 때 이 신호를 받도록 로직을 구성해야 합니다.
                if (_retreatStartTime < 0) _retreatStartTime = currentTime;
                break;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: CameraShake.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using System.Collections;

public class CameraShake : MonoBehaviour
{
    public void Shake(float amplitude, float duration)
    {
        StopAllCoroutines();
        StartCoroutine(ShakeCoroutine(amplitude, duration));
    }

    IEnumerator ShakeCoroutine(float amplitude, float duration)
    {
        Vector3 originalPos = transform.localPosition;
        float timer = 0f;
        while (timer < duration)
        {
            float x = Random.Range(-1f, 1f) * amplitude;
            float y = Random.Range(-1f, 1f) * amplitude;
            transform.localPosition = originalPos + new Vector3(x, y, 0);
            timer += Time.deltaTime;
            yield return null;
        }
        transform.localPosition = originalPos;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: DynamicCamLookPivot.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

// ===== 변수 헤더(한글 설명) =====
// 플레이어(타겟) 앞쪽에 "카메라가 바라볼 피벗"을 자동으로 위치시켜주는 보조 스크립트입니다.
// - forwardDistance: 타겟 정면 앞으로 얼마나 둘지(미터)
// - heightOffset: 타겟 기준 얼마나 높게 둘지(미터)
// - lateralOffset: 좌/우로 얼마나 치울지(미터, +는 오른쪽, -는 왼쪽)
// - followLerp: 따라붙는 속도(0=즉시 텔레포트, 1=매우 느림; 보통 0.1~0.3)
// - alignYawToTarget: 피벗의 Yaw(수평 회전)를 타겟 정면으로 맞출지 여부
// - yawLerp: Yaw 회전 보간 속도(0=즉시, 1=매우 느림)
[ExecuteAlways]
public class DynamicCamLookPivot : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("① 타겟(보통 Player)")]
    public Transform target;

    [Header("② 오프셋(미터) | 전방/높이/좌우")]
    public float forwardDistance = 2.0f;
    public float heightOffset = 1.5f;
    public float lateralOffset = 0.0f;

    [Header("③ 따라붙는 속도(0~1) | 0=즉시, 0.1~0.3 권장")]
    [Range(0f, 1f)] public float followLerp = 0.15f;

    [Header("④ 회전 옵션 | 타겟 정면으로 Yaw 정렬")]
    public bool alignYawToTarget = true;
    [Range(0f, 1f)] public float yawLerp = 0.15f;

    void LateUpdate()
    {
        if (!target) return;

        // 목표 위치 계산
        Vector3 desired = target.position
                        + target.forward * forwardDistance
                        + target.right * lateralOffset
                        + Vector3.up * heightOffset;

        // 위치 보간
        if (followLerp <= 0f || !Application.isPlaying)
            transform.position = desired;
        else
            transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(transform.position, desired, 1f - Mathf.Pow(1f - followLerp, Time.deltaTime * 60f));

        // 회전 보간(정면을 바라보게)
        if (alignYawToTarget)
        {
            Quaternion desiredRot = Quaternion.LookRotation(target.forward, Vector3.up);
            if (yawLerp <= 0f || !Application.isPlaying)
                transform.rotation = desiredRot;
            else
                transform.rotation = Quaternion.Slerp(transform.rotation, desiredRot, 1f - Mathf.Pow(1f - yawLerp, Time.deltaTime * 60f));
        }
    }
}
