using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using DG.Tweening;

public class PauseMenuView : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("UI ?곌껐")]
    public GameObject menuRoot;         
    public CanvasGroup backgroundGroup; 
    public RectTransform menuContainer; 
    
    [Header("?ㅼ젙李??곌껐")]
    public GameObject settingsPanel;
    public CanvasGroup settingsGroup;

    private void Awake()
    {
        menuRoot.SetActive(false);
        if (settingsPanel) settingsPanel.SetActive(false);
    }

    public void ShowMenu()
    {
        menuRoot.SetActive(true);
        
        // 1. 諛곌꼍: ?쒖꽌???대몢?뚯쭚 (Fade In)
        backgroundGroup.alpha = 0;
        backgroundGroup.DOFade(1, 0.3f).SetUpdate(true); 

        // 2. 硫붾돱 而⑦뀒?대꼫: ?앹뾽 ?곗텧 (?묒븯?ㅺ? 而ㅼ쭚 + ?щ챸??
        menuContainer.localScale = Vector3.one * 0.8f; // 0.8諛??ш린?먯꽌 ?쒖옉
        menuContainer.DOScale(1f, 0.3f).SetEase(Ease.OutBack).SetUpdate(true); // ?좎슜~ ?섍퀬 而ㅼ쭚
        
        CanvasGroup menuCG = menuContainer.GetComponent<CanvasGroup>();
        if (menuCG)
        {
            menuCG.alpha = 0;
            menuCG.DOFade(1, 0.3f).SetUpdate(true);
        }
    }

    public void HideMenu(System.Action onComplete = null)
    {
        // 1. 諛곌꼍: ?щ챸?댁쭚
        backgroundGroup.DOFade(0, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);

        // 2. 硫붾돱: ?묒븘吏硫댁꽌 ?щ씪吏?
        menuContainer.DOScale(0.8f, 0.2f).SetEase(Ease.InQuad).SetUpdate(true);

        CanvasGroup menuCG = menuContainer.GetComponent<CanvasGroup>();
        if (menuCG) menuCG.DOFade(0, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);

        // ?좊땲硫붿씠???앸굹硫?鍮꾪솢?깊솕
        DOVirtual.DelayedCall(0.25f, () => 
        {
            menuRoot.SetActive(false);
            onComplete?.Invoke();
        }).SetUpdate(true);
    }

    // (?ㅼ젙李??좉? ?⑥닔??湲곗〈怨??숈씪?섍쾶 ?ъ슜?섏뀛???⑸땲??
    public void ToggleSettings(bool isOpen)
    {
        // ... 湲곗〈 肄붾뱶 ?좎? ...
        if (isOpen)
        {
            settingsPanel.SetActive(true);
            settingsGroup.alpha = 0;
            settingsGroup.DOFade(1, 0.3f).SetUpdate(true);
            menuContainer.gameObject.SetActive(false); 
        }
        else
        {
            settingsGroup.DOFade(0, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true).OnComplete(() =>
            {
                settingsPanel.SetActive(false);
                menuContainer.gameObject.SetActive(true);
                
                // ?뚯븘???뚮룄 ?댁쭩 ?앹뾽 ?④낵
                menuContainer.localScale = Vector3.one * 0.9f;
                menuContainer.DOScale(1f, 0.2f).SetEase(Ease.OutBack).SetUpdate(true);
            });
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: SceneIntroAnimator.cs
// ==========================================

using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// 硫붿씤 ???쒖옉 ?? ?ㅼ젙???좊땲硫붿씠?섎뱾??
/// ?쒖꽌?濡??쇱젙 ?쒕젅?대? ?먭퀬 ?ъ깮?댁＜??而댄룷?뚰듃
/// </summary>
public class SceneIntroAnimator : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("?좊땲硫붿씠?섏씠 ?ъ깮??????좊땲硫붿씠??(?뚮젅?댁뼱 / 移대찓??/ UI ??")]
    [SerializeField] private Animator targetAnimator;

    [System.Serializable]
    public class IntroAnimation
    {
        [Header("?ъ깮???좊땲硫붿씠???곹깭/?몃━嫄??대쫫 (Animator ?대? ?대쫫)")]
        [Tooltip("Animator??State ?대쫫 ?먮뒗 Trigger ?대쫫???낅젰?⑸땲??")]
        public string stateOrTriggerName;

        [Header("?몃━嫄??ъ슜 ?щ? (true = SetTrigger, false = Play濡?吏곸젒 ?곹깭 吏꾩엯)")]
        [Tooltip("true硫?Animator.SetTrigger, false硫?Animator.Play瑜??ъ슜?⑸땲??")]
        public bool useTrigger = true;

        [Header("?ㅼ쓬 ?좊땲硫붿씠?섍퉴吏 ?湲??쒓컙 (珥??⑥쐞)")]
        [Tooltip("???좊땲硫붿씠?섏쓣 ?ㅽ뻾?????ㅼ쓬 ?④퀎源뚯? 湲곕떎由??쒓컙(珥?")]
        [Min(0f)]
        public float delayToNext = 1f;
    }

    [Header("?명듃濡??좊땲硫붿씠???쒗??(?꾩뿉???꾨옒 ?쒖꽌?濡??ъ깮)")]
    [Tooltip("???쒖옉 ???쒖꽌?濡??ъ깮???좊땲硫붿씠??紐⑸줉?낅땲??")]
    [SerializeField] private IntroAnimation[] sequence;

    [Header("???쒖옉 ???먮룞 ?ъ깮 ?щ?")]
    [Tooltip("true硫?Start()?먯꽌 ?먮룞?쇰줈 ?쒗?ㅻ? ?ъ깮?⑸땲??")]
    [SerializeField] private bool playOnStart = true;

    // ?꾩옱 ?ъ깮 以묒씤 肄붾（???몃뱾 (以묎컙???뺤?/?ъ떆???⑸룄)
    private Coroutine sequenceRoutine;

    private void Start()
    {
        // 硫붿씤 ???쒖옉怨??숈떆???먮룞 ?ъ깮 ?듭뀡??耳쒖졇 ?덈떎硫??쒗???쒖옉
        if (playOnStart)
        {
            PlaySequence();
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?명듃濡??좊땲硫붿씠???쒗?ㅻ? ?몃??먯꽌 ?섎룞?쇰줈 ?쒖옉?????몄텧
    /// (?? ?ㅻⅨ 留ㅻ땲??먯꽌 而룹뵮 ?댄썑 ?몄텧)
    /// </summary>
    [ContextMenu("Play Sequence (Test)")]
    public void PlaySequence()
    {
        if (targetAnimator == null)
        {
            Debug.LogWarning("[SceneIntroAnimator] Target Animator媛 ?ㅼ젙?섏뼱 ?덉? ?딆뒿?덈떎.");
            return;
        }

        // ?댁쟾???뚭퀬 ?덈뜕 ?쒗?ㅺ? ?덈떎硫??뺤?
        if (sequenceRoutine != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(sequenceRoutine);
        }

        // ?덈줈???쒗??肄붾（???쒖옉
        sequenceRoutine = StartCoroutine(PlaySequenceRoutine());
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?명듃濡??쒗?ㅻ? ?쒖꽌?濡??ㅽ뻾?섎뒗 ?ㅼ젣 肄붾（??
    /// </summary>
    private IEnumerator PlaySequenceRoutine()
    {
        // 諛곗뿴???ㅼ젙???쒖꽌?濡??좊땲硫붿씠???ㅽ뻾
        for (int i = 0; i < sequence.Length; i++)
        {
            IntroAnimation step = sequence[i];

            // ?대쫫??鍮꾩뼱 ?덉쑝硫??ㅽ궢 (?ㅼ닔 諛⑹???
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(step.stateOrTriggerName))
                continue;

            // ?몃━嫄?諛⑹떇 vs 吏곸젒 Play 諛⑹떇 ?좏깮
            if (step.useTrigger)
            {
                // Animator Controller?먯꽌 媛숈? ?대쫫??Trigger ?뚮씪誘명꽣媛 ?덉뼱????
                // 議댁옱?섏? ?딅뒗 ?뚮씪誘명꽣 ?대쫫?쇰줈 SetTrigger瑜?怨꾩냽 ?몄텧?섎㈃
                // ?고????먮윭???꾨땲吏留? ?붾쾭源낆씠 ?대졄怨??깅뒫 ??퉬媛 ?????덉쓬.
                targetAnimator.SetTrigger(step.stateOrTriggerName);
            }
            else
            {
                // State ?대쫫?쇰줈 吏곸젒 吏꾩엯
                // ??寃쎌슦 Layer, NormalizedTime ?깆쓣 ?몃? 議곗젙?섍퀬 ?띠쑝硫?
                // ?ㅻ쾭濡쒕뱶(Play(string stateName, int layer, float time)) ?ъ슜 媛??
                targetAnimator.Play(step.stateOrTriggerName);
            }

            // ?ㅼ쓬 ?④퀎源뚯? ?湲?
            if (step.delayToNext > 0f)
            {
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(step.delayToNext);
            }
            else
            {
                // 0 ?댄븯??寃쎌슦 ???꾨젅?꾩? ?섍린怨?諛붾줈 ?ㅼ쓬 ?④퀎濡?
                yield return null;
            }
        }

        // ?쒗?ㅺ? ?앸궗?쇰?濡?肄붾（???몃뱾 ?뺣━
        sequenceRoutine = null;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ?ъ깮 以묒씤 ?쒗?ㅻ? 利됱떆 以묐떒?섍퀬 ?띠쓣 ???ъ슜
    /// (?? ?뚮젅?댁뼱媛 ?ㅽ궢 踰꾪듉???뚮?????
    /// </summary>
    public void StopSequence()
    {
        if (sequenceRoutine != null)
        {
            StopCoroutine(sequenceRoutine);
            sequenceRoutine = null;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: SoulButton.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using TMPro;
using DG.Tweening;

public class SoulButton : MonoBehaviour, IPointerEnterHandler, IPointerExitHandler, IPointerClickHandler
{
    [Header("??UI ?곌껐")]
    public TextMeshProUGUI buttonText;
    public Image iconImage;

    [Header("???곗텧 ?ㅼ젙")]
    public Color normalColor = Color.white; // ??湲곕낯媛??곗깋?쇰줈 蹂寃?
    public Color hoverColor = new Color(1f, 0.8f, 0f, 1f); 
    public float scaleAmount = 1.1f;

    private void Start()
    {
        if (buttonText) buttonText.color = normalColor;
        if (iconImage) iconImage.DOFade(0, 0).SetUpdate(true);
    }

    public void OnPointerEnter(PointerEventData eventData)
    {
        // ??SetUpdate(true) ?꾩닔
        if (buttonText) buttonText.DOColor(hoverColor, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);
        transform.DOScale(scaleAmount, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);
        if (iconImage) iconImage.DOFade(1, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);
    }

    public void OnPointerExit(PointerEventData eventData)
    {
        if (buttonText) buttonText.DOColor(normalColor, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);
        transform.DOScale(1f, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);
        if (iconImage) iconImage.DOFade(0, 0.2f).SetUpdate(true);
    }

    public void OnPointerClick(PointerEventData eventData)
    {
        transform.DOPunchScale(new Vector3(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.1f), 0.1f).SetUpdate(true);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: SystemBootLoader.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

/// 遺??濡쒓렇瑜??곕??먯쿂???꾩뿉???꾨옒濡??섎젮蹂대궡怨?
/// 紐⑤뱺 濡쒓렇媛 ?앸궃 ?ㅼ뿉留?PRESS ANY KEY瑜?蹂댁뿬二쇰ŉ,
/// PRESS ANY KEY ?꾩튂 洹쇱쿂??SYSTEM READY... N% 吏꾪뻾瑜좎쓣 ?④퍡 ?쒖떆?쒕떎.
/// </summary>
public class SystemBootLoader : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("??湲곕낯 ?ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("遺?????대룞???ㅼ쓬 ???대쫫")]
    public string nextSceneName = "LobbyScene";

    [Tooltip("濡쒓렇 ??以꾧낵 ??以??ъ씠??媛꾧꺽(珥?. ?묒쓣?섎줉 ??鍮⑤━ ?대젮??)]
    public float typingSpeed = 0.02f;

    [Header("??濡쒓렇 ?곗텧 ?ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("?붾㈃???숈떆??蹂댁씠??理쒕? 濡쒓렇 以???)]
    [Range(1, 30)]
    public int maxVisibleLines = 10;              // ?곕???李쎌뿉 ??긽 蹂댁씠??以???湲곕낯 10以?

    [Header("??UI ?곌껐")]
    [Tooltip("濡쒓렇 異쒕젰???띿뒪??(硫?곕씪??")]
    public TextMeshProUGUI logText;               // 濡쒓렇 異쒕젰???띿뒪??

    [Tooltip("吏꾪뻾瑜?% ?띿뒪??(?곷떒 ??")]
    public TextMeshProUGUI progressText;          // 吏꾪뻾瑜?% ?띿뒪??

    [Tooltip("遺??吏꾪뻾 諛?)]
    public Slider progressBar;                    // 吏꾪뻾瑜?諛?

    [Tooltip("?꾨Т ?ㅻ굹 ?꾨Ⅴ?쇰뒗 硫붿떆吏???띿뒪??)]
    public TextMeshProUGUI pressAnyKeyText;       // PRESS ANY KEY ?띿뒪??

    [Header("??READY ?곹깭 UI")]
    [Tooltip("READY ?쇱꽱?몃? 蹂댁뿬以??띿뒪??(PRESS ANY KEY 洹쇱쿂??諛곗튂)")]
    public TextMeshProUGUI readyStatusText;       // SYSTEM READY... N% ?쒖떆???띿뒪??

    [Header("??遺??濡쒓렇 ?댁슜 (湲멸쾶 ?ｌ뼱????")]
    [TextArea(2, 10)]
    public string[] bootLogs = new string[]
    {

    // ?대? ?곹깭
    private string finalLogState;                        // ?꾩껜 濡쒓렇 ?띿뒪???꾩슂 ???ъ궗??
    private readonly Queue<string> visibleLines = new Queue<string>(); // ?붾㈃??蹂댁씠??濡쒓렇 踰꾪띁

    void Awake()
    {
        // PRESS ANY KEY???쒖옉 ??臾댁“嫄??④?
        if (pressAnyKeyText != null)
        {
            pressAnyKeyText.gameObject.SetActive(false);

            // ?뚰뙆 珥덇린???뱀떆 ?댁쟾 ?몄쐢 ?곹뼢 ?쒓굅)
            Color c = pressAnyKeyText.color;
            c.a = 1f;
            pressAnyKeyText.color = c;
        }

        // READY ?쇱꽱???띿뒪??珥덇린??
        if (readyStatusText != null)
        {
            readyStatusText.text = string.Empty;
        }

        // 濡쒓렇 ?띿뒪??珥덇린??
        if (logText != null)
            logText.text = string.Empty;
    }

    void Start()
    {
        StartCoroutine(BootSequence());
    }

    IEnumerator BootSequence()
    {
        // 1. 鍮꾨룞湲???濡쒕뵫 ?쒖옉 (?먮룞 ?꾪솚? 留됱븘??
        AsyncOperation op = SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(nextSceneName);
        op.allowSceneActivation = false;

        StringBuilder fullLogBuilder = new StringBuilder();
        int totalLogs = bootLogs.Length;

        visibleLines.Clear();

        // 2. 濡쒓렇瑜???以꾩뵫 ?꾩뿉???꾨옒濡?"?대젮?ㅻ벏" 異쒕젰
        for (int i = 0; i < totalLogs; i++)
        {
            string line = bootLogs[i];

            // ?꾩껜 濡쒓렇???꾩쟻 (?꾩슂?섎떎硫??섏쨷???꾩껜 濡쒓렇 ?쒖떆??
            fullLogBuilder.AppendLine(line);
            finalLogState = fullLogBuilder.ToString();

            // ?붾㈃??蹂댁씠???먯뿉 異붽?
            visibleLines.Enqueue(line);
            // maxVisibleLines蹂대떎 留롮븘吏硫?媛???ㅻ옒??以꾨????쒓굅 ???곕??먯뿉???꾨줈 諛由щ뒗 ?먮굦
            while (visibleLines.Count > maxVisibleLines)
            {
                visibleLines.Dequeue();
            }

            if (logText != null)
            {
                // ?먯뿉 ?ㅼ뼱?덈뒗 寃껊쭔 ?ㅼ떆 ?댁뼱 遺숈뿬??蹂댁뿬以?
                logText.text = string.Join("\n", visibleLines.ToArray());
            }

            // 濡쒓렇 吏꾪뻾??(0~1)
            float progress = (float)(i + 1) / totalLogs;

            // ?곷떒 吏꾪뻾瑜?UI 媛깆떊
            if (progressBar != null)
                progressBar.value = progress;

            if (progressText != null)
                progressText.text = $"SYSTEM BOOT... {(progress * 100f):0}%";

            // PRESS ANY KEY ?먮━ 洹쇱쿂 READY ?쇱꽱???쒖떆
            if (readyStatusText != null)
            {
                // 濡쒓렇 吏꾪뻾?꾩뿉 留욎떠 SYSTEM READY... N% 媛깆떊
                readyStatusText.text = $"SYSTEM READY... {(progress * 100f):0}%";
            }

            // 以?媛?媛꾧꺽(?쒕뜡 ?쎄컙 ?욎뼱??湲곌퀎 濡쒓렇 ?먮굦)
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(Random.Range(typingSpeed * 0.5f, typingSpeed * 1.5f));
        }

        // 3. ?꾨즺 ?곹깭 ?쒖떆 (100%)
        if (progressText != null)
            progressText.text = "SYSTEM BOOT... 100%";

        if (progressBar != null)
            progressBar.value = 1.0f;

        if (readyStatusText != null)
            readyStatusText.text = "SYSTEM READY... 100%";

        // 4. ?ㅼ젣 濡쒕뵫???앸궇 ?뚭퉴吏 ?湲?(?ъ? ?꾩쭅 ?꾪솚?섏? ?딆쓬)
        while (op.progress < 0.9f)
        {
            yield return null;
        }

        // 5. 紐⑤뱺 濡쒓렇媛 ?앸궃 ?꾩뿉留?PRESS ANY KEY ?깆옣 + ?낅젰 ?湲?
        yield return StartCoroutine(WaitForPlayerInputAndProceed(op));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ???낅젰 ?쒖뒪??援??낅젰 ?쒖뒪?????ㅼ뿉??"?꾨Т ?? ?낅젰 媛먯?.
    /// </summary>
    private bool IsAnyKeyPressedThisFrame()
    {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
        if (Keyboard.current != null && Keyboard.current.anyKey.wasPressedThisFrame)
            return true;
#endif
        return Input.anyKeyDown;
    }

    // ?꾨Т ???낅젰 ?湲?濡쒖쭅
    IEnumerator WaitForPlayerInputAndProceed(AsyncOperation op)
    {
        // ?몄뒪?숉꽣?먯꽌 pressAnyKeyText ?곌껐 ???섏뼱 ?덉쑝硫?
        // ?먮룞?쇰줈 ?섏뼱媛??섎컰???놁쓬 ??諛섎뱶???좊떦????寃?
        if (pressAnyKeyText == null)
        {
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(1.0f);
            op.allowSceneActivation = true;
            yield break;
        }

        // ?뱀떆 ?댁쟾 ???낅젰???⑥븘?덉쓣 ???덉쑝??吏㏐쾶 踰꾪띁 ?뚮윭??
        float flushTime = 0.15f;
        while (flushTime > 0f)
        {
            flushTime -= Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            yield return null;
        }

        // ?ш린?쒕???PRESS ANY KEY ?쒖떆 (濡쒓렇 + 濡쒕뵫 ?꾨즺 ??
        pressAnyKeyText.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        pressAnyKeyText.text = "PRESS ANY KEY . . .";

        // ?먮㈇(?뚰뙆 1 ??0.3) 諛섎났
        Tween blinkTween = pressAnyKeyText
            .DOFade(0.3f, 0.5f)
            .SetLoops(-1, LoopType.Yoyo)
            .SetUpdate(true); // timeScale ?곹뼢 ??諛쏄쾶

        // READY ?쇱꽱?몃뒗 ?대? 100%濡?怨좎젙???곹깭?먯꽌,
        // ?뚮젅?댁뼱 ?ㅼ젣 ?낅젰??湲곕떎由?
        while (!IsAnyKeyPressedThisFrame())
        {
            yield return null;
        }

        // ?먮㈇ 醫낅즺 諛??④?
        blinkTween.Kill();
        pressAnyKeyText.gameObject.SetActive(false);

        // 6. ?섏씠???꾩썐 ?????쒖꽦??
        if (SceneFader.Instance != null && SceneFader.Instance.fadeCanvasGroup != null)
        {
            SceneFader.Instance.fadeCanvasGroup.blocksRaycasts = true;
            SceneFader.Instance.fadeCanvasGroup
                .DOFade(1f, 1.0f)
                .OnComplete(() =>
                {
                    op.allowSceneActivation = true;
                });
        }
        else
        {
            op.allowSceneActivation = true;
        }
    }
}

// Assets/Scripts/UI/TitleSceneUIController.cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class TitleSceneUIController : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("寃뚯엫 ?쒖옉 ??濡쒕뱶?????대쫫")]
    [SerializeField]
    private string gameSceneName = "BootScene"; // ?ㅼ젣 ???대쫫?쇰줈 援먯껜

    [Header("??濡쒕뱶 ?꾩뿉 Time.timeScale 珥덇린?뷀븷吏 ?щ?")]
    [SerializeField]
    private bool resetTimeScaleOnLoad = true;

    // 踰꾪듉 OnClick?먯꽌 ?몄텧???⑥닔
    public void OnClick_StartGame()
    {
        if (resetTimeScaleOnLoad && Time.timeScale != 1f)
            Time.timeScale = 1f;

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(gameSceneName))
    {
        Debug.LogError("[TitleSceneUIController] gameSceneName ??鍮꾩뼱 ?덉뒿?덈떎.", this);
        return;
    }

    // ??[?듭떖] SceneFader瑜??듯빐 "BootScene"?쇰줈 ?대룞?댁빞 ?⑸땲??
    if (SceneFader.Instance != null)
    {
        SceneFader.Instance.FadeOutAndLoadScene(gameSceneName); // gameSceneName == "BootScene"
    }
    else
    {
        // SceneFader媛 ?놁쓣 ???鍮?(鍮꾩긽??
        SceneManager.LoadScene(gameSceneName, LoadSceneMode.Single); 
    }
}}

// ==========================================
// File: UIButtonHoverAnimator.cs
// ==========================================

// Assets/Scripts/UIButtonHoverAnimator.cs
using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class UIButtonHoverAnimator : MonoBehaviour, IPointerEnterHandler, IPointerExitHandler
{
    [Header("??洹몃９ (RectTransform ?먮뒗 ?쇰컲 GameObject 媛??")]
    [Tooltip("踰꾪듉怨?諛곌꼍???먯떇?쇰줈 ??遺紐? UI??寃쎌슦 RectTransform, ?쇰컲 ?ㅻ툕?앺듃??媛??")]
    public Transform groupTransform;

    [Header("??湲濡쒖슦")]
    public GameObject glowObject;

    [Header("???좊땲硫붿씠???쒕떇")]
    public Vector2 hoverOffset = new Vector2(20f, 0f);
    public float duration = 0.18f;
    public AnimationCurve easeCurve = default;
    public bool disableGlowOnExit = true;

    // ?대?
    RectTransform _rect;
    Vector2 _originalAnchoredPos;
    Vector3 _originalLocalPos;
    Image _glowImage;
    Coroutine _moveCoroutine;
    Coroutine _glowCoroutine;

    void Reset() { if (easeCurve == null || easeCurve.length == 0) easeCurve = AnimationCurve.EaseInOut(0, 0, 1, 1); }

    void Awake()
    {
        if (groupTransform == null) groupTransform = GetComponent<Transform>();
        _rect = groupTransform as RectTransform;
        if (_rect != null)
            _originalAnchoredPos = _rect.anchoredPosition;
        else
            _originalLocalPos = groupTransform.localPosition;

        if (glowObject != null)
        {
            _glowImage = glowObject.GetComponent<Image>();
            if (_glowImage != null) _glowImage.raycastTarget = false;
            glowObject.SetActive(false);
        }
    }

    public void OnPointerEnter(PointerEventData eventData)
    {
        Vector2 targetAnchored = _originalAnchoredPos + hoverOffset;
        Vector3 targetLocal = _originalLocalPos + new Vector3(hoverOffset.x, hoverOffset.y, 0f);

        if (_moveCoroutine != null) StopCoroutine(_moveCoroutine);
        _moveCoroutine = StartCoroutine(AnimateMove((_rect != null) ? (Vector3)targetAnchored : targetLocal, duration));

        if (glowObject != null)
        {
            glowObject.SetActive(true);
            if (_glowImage != null)
            {
                if (_glowCoroutine != null) StopCoroutine(_glowCoroutine);
                _glowCoroutine = StartCoroutine(AnimateGlowAlpha(0f, 1f, duration));
            }
        }
    }

    public void OnPointerExit(PointerEventData eventData)
    {
        Vector2 targetAnchored = _originalAnchoredPos;
        Vector3 targetLocal = _originalLocalPos;

        if (_moveCoroutine != null) StopCoroutine(_moveCoroutine);
        _moveCoroutine = StartCoroutine(AnimateMove((_rect != null) ? (Vector3)targetAnchored : targetLocal, duration));

        if (glowObject != null && _glowImage != null)
        {
            if (_glowCoroutine != null) StopCoroutine(_glowCoroutine);
            _glowCoroutine = StartCoroutine(AnimateGlowAlpha(_glowImage.color.a, 0f, duration, () =>
            {
                if (disableGlowOnExit && glowObject != null) glowObject.SetActive(false);
            }));
        }
        else if (glowObject != null && disableGlowOnExit)
        {
            glowObject.SetActive(false);
        }
    }

    IEnumerator AnimateMove(Vector3 target, float time)
    {
        float t = 0f;
        if (_rect != null)
        {
            Vector2 start = _rect.anchoredPosition;
            Vector2 end = (Vector2)target;
            while (t < time)
            {
                t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                float p = Mathf.Clamp01(t / time);
                float e = easeCurve.Evaluate(p);
                _rect.anchoredPosition = Vector2.LerpUnclamped(start, end, e);
                yield return null;
            }
            _rect.anchoredPosition = end;
        }
        else
        {
            Vector3 start = groupTransform.localPosition;
            Vector3 end = target;
            while (t < time)
            {
                t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                float p = Mathf.Clamp01(t / time);
                float e = easeCurve.Evaluate(p);
                groupTransform.localPosition = Vector3.LerpUnclamped(start, end, e);
                yield return null;
            }
            groupTransform.localPosition = end;
        }
    }

    IEnumerator AnimateGlowAlpha(float from, float to, float time, System.Action onComplete = null)
    {
        if (_glowImage == null) { onComplete?.Invoke(); yield break; }
        float t = 0f;
        Color col = _glowImage.color;
        while (t < time)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            float p = Mathf.Clamp01(t / time);
            float e = easeCurve.Evaluate(p);
            col.a = Mathf.LerpUnclamped(from, to, e);
            _glowImage.color = col;
            yield return null;
        }
        col.a = to;
        _glowImage.color = col;
        onComplete?.Invoke();
    }

    void OnValidate()
    {
        if (glowObject != null)
        {
            var img = glowObject.GetComponent<Image>();
            if (img != null) img.raycastTarget = false;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: QuestTrigger.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

public class QuestTrigger : MonoBehaviour
{
    private bool isTriggered = false;

    private void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
    {
        if (isTriggered) return;

        // ?뚮젅?댁뼱 ?쒓렇 ?뺤씤
        if (other.CompareTag("Player"))
        {
            isTriggered = true;
            Debug.Log("紐⑺몴 吏???꾩갑!");

            // ?쒗넗由ъ뼹 留ㅻ땲??먭쾶 ?뚮┝
            if (TutorialManager.Instance != null)
            {
                TutorialManager.Instance.OnGoalReached();
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: TutorialDummy.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using DG.Tweening; // ?寃⑷컧 ?곗텧??

// AttackHitbox媛 李얠쓣 ???덈룄濡?IDamageReceiver瑜?諛섎뱶??援ы쁽?댁빞 ?⑸땲??
public class TutorialDummy : MonoBehaviour, IDamageReceiver
{
    [Header("?ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("泥대젰??源롮씠???곗텧???꾪븳 ?붾뱾由?媛뺣룄")]
    public float shakeStrength = 0.5f;
    
    [Tooltip("?쇨꺽 ?댄럺??(?좏깮)")]
    public GameObject hitEffectPrefab;

    // 怨듦꺽 荑⑦???(?덈Т 鍮좊Ⅴ寃??ㅻ떒?덊듃 ?섎뒗 寃?諛⑹???
    private float lastHitTime;
    private float hitCooldown = 0.1f;

    /// <summary>
    /// AttackHitbox.cs?먯꽌 ?몄텧?섎뒗 ?⑥닔?낅땲??
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="payload">怨듦꺽 ?뺣낫(?곕?吏, ?꾩튂 ??</param>
    public void ReceiveHit(HitPayload payload)
    {
        // 1. 荑⑦???泥댄겕 (吏㏃? ?쒓컙???덈Т 留롮씠 ?몄텧?섎뒗 寃?諛⑹?)
        if (Time.time - lastHitTime < hitCooldown) return;
        lastHitTime = Time.time;

        // 2. ?寃⑷컧 ?곗텧 (DOTween Shake)
        // 湲곗〈 ?몄쐢???덉쑝硫?硫덉텛怨??덈줈 ?쒖옉 (遺?먯뿰?ㅻ윭???⑤┝ 諛⑹?)
        transform.DOKill(true); 
        transform.DOShakeScale(0.2f, shakeStrength, 10, 90, true);

        // 3. ?쇨꺽 ?댄럺???앹꽦
        if (hitEffectPrefab != null)
        {
            Instantiate(hitEffectPrefab, payload.hitPoint, Quaternion.LookRotation(payload.hitDirection));
        }

        Debug.Log($"[Dummy] Hit by {payload.attacker?.name}, Damage: {payload.damage}");

        // 4. ?쒗넗由ъ뼹 留ㅻ땲??먭쾶 "??留욎븯??" 蹂닿퀬
        if (TutorialManager.Instance != null)
        {
            TutorialManager.Instance.OnEnemyHit();
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: TutorialManager.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using TMPro;
using DG.Tweening;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public enum TutorialStep
{
    None, Movement, Attack, Defense, Heal, Complete
}

public class TutorialManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static TutorialManager Instance;

    [Header("????붿갹 UI")]
    public TextMeshProUGUI centerTitleText;    // 以묒븰 ??댄? ?띿뒪??
    public CanvasGroup dialogueGroup;
    public TextMeshProUGUI speakerText;
    public TextMeshProUGUI contentText;

    [Header("???섏뒪??UI")]
    public CanvasGroup questPanelGroup;        // ?섏뒪???꾩껜 ?⑤꼸 (諛곌꼍 ?ы븿)
    public Image questPanelBg;                 // ?섏뒪???⑤꼸 諛곌꼍 ?대?吏
    public TextMeshProUGUI questTitleText;
    public TextMeshProUGUI questDescriptionText;
    public GameObject comboGuidePanel;

    [Header("??寃뚯엫 ?ㅻ툕?앺듃")]
    public GameObject movementGoal;
    public GameObject attackDummy;
    public GameObject droneEnemy;
    public PlayerHealth playerHealth;

    [Header("??????ㅼ젙")]
    [Tooltip("???媛꾧꺽 (珥?")]
    public float introDelay = 2.0f;
    public float moveDelay = 2.0f;
    public float attackDelay = 2.0f;
    public float defenseDelay = 2.5f;
    public float healDelay = 2.0f;
    public float completeDelay = 3.0f;

    [Tooltip("湲????댄븨 ?띾룄")]
    [Range(0.01f, 0.2f)]
    public float textTypingSpeed = 0.05f;

    [Header("???덈젴 ?ㅼ젙")]
    public int targetDodgeCount = 2;
    public int targetGuardCount = 3;
    public int targetParryCount = 1;

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // ?곹깭 蹂??(?대? 濡쒖쭅??
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    private TutorialStep currentStep = TutorialStep.None;
    private int currentDodgeCount = 0;
    private bool isGoalReached = false;
    private int currentAttackCount = 0;
    private int maxAttackCount = 4;
    private int currentGuardCount = 0;
    private int currentParryCount = 0;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance == null) Instance = this;
        else Destroy(gameObject);
    }

    void Start()
    {
        // 以묒븰 ??댄? 珥덇린??
        if (centerTitleText)
        {
            centerTitleText.text = "";
            centerTitleText.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }

        // ??붿갹? 泥섏쓬????蹂댁씠寃?
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.alpha = 0;

        // ?섏뒪???⑤꼸:
        //  - ?몄뒪?숉꽣???ㅼ젙???뚰뙆/?됱긽??洹몃?濡??ъ슜?쒕떎.
        //  - ?곹샇?묒슜/?덉씠罹먯뒪?몃쭔 鍮꾪솢??(?⑥닚 ?뺣낫???⑤꼸?대?濡?
        if (questPanelGroup)
        {
            questPanelGroup.interactable = false;
            questPanelGroup.blocksRaycasts = false;
            // questPanelGroup.alpha 瑜??ш린??嫄대뱶由ъ? ?딆쓬
        }
        // questPanelBg.color ???몄뒪?숉꽣 媛?洹몃?濡??ъ슜 (媛뺤젣 ??뼱?곌린 ?쒓굅)

        // ?ㅻ툕?앺듃 ?④린湲?
        if (comboGuidePanel) comboGuidePanel.SetActive(false);
        if (movementGoal) movementGoal.SetActive(false);
        if (attackDummy) attackDummy.SetActive(false);
        if (droneEnemy) droneEnemy.SetActive(false);

        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Intro());
    }

    void Update()
    {
        if (currentStep == TutorialStep.Movement)
        {
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.LeftShift)) OnDodgeAction();
        }
        if (currentStep == TutorialStep.Heal)
        {
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Q)) OnHealAction();
        }
    }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // ?쒗??
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator Sequence_Intro()
    {
        // 以묒븰 ??댄? ?곗텧 ?쒖옉
        if (centerTitleText)
        {
            centerTitleText.text = "媛?곹쁽??遺???꾨즺.";
            centerTitleText.DOFade(1, 0.5f);
        }

        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "媛?곹쁽??遺???꾨즺.", introDelay));

        if (centerTitleText) centerTitleText.DOFade(0, 0.5f); // 以묒븰 ??댄? ?щ씪吏?

        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "?댁씪 ?몄슦??媛湲???留덉?留??덈젴??吏꾪뻾??蹂댁옄怨?", introDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "?뚭쿋?? ?鍮꾨뒗 泥좎????섎뒗寃?醫뗪쿋吏.", introDelay));

     
        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Movement());
    }

    IEnumerator Sequence_Movement()
    {
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "???곗꽑 紐?醫 ??대낵源? WASD濡??吏곸뿬蹂쇰옒?", moveDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "醫뗭븘, ?대쾲??留덉슦?ㅻ? ?吏곸뿬 二쇱쐞瑜??섎윭遊?", moveDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "????앹쓣 吏묒쨷?댁꽌 蹂대젮硫?留덉슦???좎쓣 ?낅젰??怨좎젙 ?????덉뼱.", moveDelay));

        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);

        currentStep = TutorialStep.Movement;
        if (movementGoal) movementGoal.SetActive(true);

        PunchEffect();
        UpdateMovementUI();
    }

    void OnDodgeAction()
    {
        if (currentDodgeCount < targetDodgeCount)
        {
            currentDodgeCount++;
            PunchEffect();
            UpdateMovementUI();
            CheckMovementComplete();
        }
    }

    public void OnGoalReached()
    {
        if (!isGoalReached)
        {
            isGoalReached = true;
            PunchEffect();
            UpdateMovementUI();
            CheckMovementComplete();
        }
    }

    void UpdateMovementUI()
    {
        string goalStr = isGoalReached ? "<color=green>(?꾨즺)</color>" : "(誘몄셿猷?";
        UpdateQuestUI("湲곗큹 湲곕룞", $"1. 吏?뺣맂 ?꾩튂濡??대룞 {goalStr}\n2. Shift ?뚰뵾 ({currentDodgeCount}/{targetDodgeCount})");
    }

    void CheckMovementComplete()
    {
        if (isGoalReached && currentDodgeCount >= targetDodgeCount)
        {
            if (movementGoal) movementGoal.SetActive(false);
            StartCoroutine(Sequence_Attack());
        }
    }

    IEnumerator Sequence_Attack()
    {
        currentStep = TutorialStep.None;
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "紐몄씠 醫 ?由?寃?媛숇꽕! ?댁젣 寃???섎몮?щ낫??", attackDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "留덉슦??醫뚰겢由?쑝濡??쎄났寃? ?고겢由?쑝濡?媛뺢났寃⑹쓣 ?????덉뼱.", attackDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "?ㅼ????대쾲??肄ㅻ낫瑜??ъ슜?대낫?? ?쎄났寃⑹쓣 4踰??곗냽 ?낅젰?섎㈃ ??", attackDelay));

        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);
        if (comboGuidePanel)
        {
            comboGuidePanel.SetActive(true);
            comboGuidePanel.transform.DOPunchScale(Vector3.one * 0.1f, 0.3f);
        }
        currentStep = TutorialStep.Attack;
        if (attackDummy) attackDummy.SetActive(true);

        PunchEffect();
        UpdateQuestUI("?꾪닾 ?덈젴", $"?덉닔?꾨퉬瑜?怨듦꺽?섏꽭??\n({currentAttackCount} / {maxAttackCount})");
    }

    public void OnEnemyHit()
    {
        if (currentStep != TutorialStep.Attack) return;

        currentAttackCount++;
        UpdateQuestUI("?꾪닾 ?덈젴", $"?덉닔?꾨퉬瑜?怨듦꺽?섏꽭??\n({currentAttackCount} / {maxAttackCount})");
        PunchEffect();

        if (currentAttackCount >= maxAttackCount)
        {
            if (attackDummy) attackDummy.SetActive(false);
            if (comboGuidePanel) comboGuidePanel.SetActive(false);
            StartCoroutine(Sequence_MidTalk());
        }
    }

    IEnumerator Sequence_MidTalk()
    {
        currentStep = TutorialStep.None;
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "?ㅻ뒛? 而⑤뵒?섏씠 醫뗫꽕, 寃??媛踰쇱썙.", attackDelay));
        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Defense());
    }

    IEnumerator Sequence_Defense()
    {
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "?대쾲??諛⑹뼱瑜??곗뒿?대낫?? E瑜???쒗빐 媛???????덉뼱.", defenseDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "??긽 諛⑹뼱留??????놁?! ??대컢??留욎떠 媛???섎㈃ ?⑤쭅??諛쒕룞??", defenseDelay));
        if (droneEnemy) droneEnemy.SetActive(true);
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "媛꾨떒?섍쾶 紐?踰??곗뒿?대낫?먭퀬.", defenseDelay));
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);

        currentStep = TutorialStep.Defense;
        PunchEffect();
        UpdateDefenseUI();
    }

    public void OnPlayerGuardSuccess()
    {
        if (currentStep != TutorialStep.Defense) return;
        if (currentGuardCount < targetGuardCount)
        {
            currentGuardCount++;
            PunchEffect();
            UpdateDefenseUI();
            CheckDefenseComplete();
        }
    }

    public void OnPlayerParrySuccess()
    {
        if (currentStep != TutorialStep.Defense) return;
        if (currentParryCount < targetParryCount)
        {
            currentParryCount++;
            PunchEffect();
            UpdateDefenseUI();
            CheckDefenseComplete();
        }
    }

    void UpdateDefenseUI()
    {
        UpdateQuestUI("諛⑹뼱 ?덈젴",
            $"?쒕줎 怨듦꺽 諛⑹뼱\n1. 媛??({currentGuardCount}/{targetGuardCount})\n2. ?⑤쭅 ({currentParryCount}/{targetParryCount})");
    }

    void CheckDefenseComplete()
    {
        if (currentGuardCount >= targetGuardCount && currentParryCount >= targetParryCount)
        {
            if (droneEnemy) droneEnemy.SetActive(false);
            StartCoroutine(Sequence_Heal());
        }
    }

    IEnumerator Sequence_Heal()
    {
        currentStep = TutorialStep.None;
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "?ㅽ뙣?섎㈃ ?꾪뿕?섍쿋?? ??媛먭컖???딆?留먯옄.", healDelay));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "泥대젰 ?뚮났? Q瑜??낅젰???뚮났 ?고뵆???ъ슜?섏뿬 ?뚮났 ?????덉뼱.", healDelay));
       

        currentStep = TutorialStep.Heal;
        if (playerHealth) playerHealth.ApplyDamage(50);

        PunchEffect();
        UpdateQuestUI("?뚮났 ?덈젴", "泥대젰???먯떎?섏뿀?듬땲??\n'Q' ?ㅻ? ?뚮윭 ?고뵆???ъ슜?섏꽭??");
    }

    void OnHealAction()
    {
        if (playerHealth) playerHealth.Heal(50);
        PunchEffect();
        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Complete());
    }

    IEnumerator Sequence_Complete()
    {
        currentStep = TutorialStep.Complete;
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "醫뗭븘! ?덈젴? 紐⑤몢 ?앸궗?? ?댁젣 ?ㅼ쟾?대떎.", completeDelay));
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);

        UpdateQuestUI("?쒗넗由ъ뼹 ?꾨즺", "紐⑤뱺 ?덈젴??留덉낀?듬땲??\n?섍퀬?섏뀲?듬땲??");
        PunchEffect();
    }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // ?ы띁 ?⑥닔
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator PlayDialogue(string speaker, string content, float waitTime)
    {
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.alpha = 1;
        if (speakerText) speakerText.text = speaker;

        if (contentText)
        {
            contentText.text = "";
            foreach (char c in content)
            {
                contentText.text += c;
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(textTypingSpeed);
            }
        }
        yield return new WaitForSeconds(waitTime);
    }

    void ShowQuestPanel()
    {
        // ?꾩슂?섎㈃ ?섏쨷???뱀젙 ??대컢???몄텧?댁꽌 媛뺤“ ?곗텧?⑹쑝濡??ъ슜
        if (questPanelGroup)
        {
            questPanelGroup.alpha = 1;   // 蹂댁씠寃??좎?
            questPanelGroup.interactable = true;
            questPanelGroup.transform.DOPunchScale(Vector3.one * 0.15f, 0.5f);
        }
    }

    void UpdateQuestUI(string title, string desc)
    {
        if (questTitleText) questTitleText.text = title;
        if (questDescriptionText) questDescriptionText.text = desc;
    }

    void PunchEffect()
    {
        if (questPanelGroup)
            questPanelGroup.transform.DOPunchScale(Vector3.one * 0.05f, 0.2f);
    }
}

// File: BranchTrigger.cs

using UnityEngine;

public class BranchTrigger : MonoBehaviour
{
    private bool isTriggered = false;

    void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
    {
        if (isTriggered) return;

        if (other.CompareTag("Player"))
        {
            isTriggered = true;
            
            // 留ㅻ땲??먭쾶 "媛덈┝湲??좏깮 ?꾨즺" ?좏샇 蹂대깂
            if (LobbyManager.Instance != null)
            {
                LobbyManager.Instance.OnBranchSelected();
            }

            // (?좏깮 ?ы빆) 諛잛? ?몃━嫄곕뒗 ?꾧린
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

public class ElevatorPanel : BaseInteractable
{
    public override bool TryInteract(object invoker = null)
    {
        if (LobbyManager.Instance != null)
        {
            // ?곹샇?묒슜 ?깃났 -> 留ㅻ땲?媛 ???대룞 泥섎━
            LobbyManager.Instance.OnInteractElevator();
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: GuidePathSystem.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class GuidePathSystem : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("???ㅼ젙")]
    public Transform player;       
    [Tooltip("LineRenderer媛 遺숈? ?꾨━?뱀쓣 ?ｌ쑝?몄슂")]
    public GameObject linePrefab; // ???꾨━???곌껐 蹂??
    
    public float pathHeight = 0.5f; 
    public float textureScrollSpeed = 2.0f; 

    // ?쒖꽦?붾맂 ?쇱씤??愿由?
    private List<LineRenderer> activeLines = new List<LineRenderer>();
    private List<Transform> currentTargets = new List<Transform>();
    private NavMeshPath navMeshPath;

    void Awake()
    {
        navMeshPath = new NavMeshPath();
        
        if (player == null)
        {
            var p = GameObject.FindWithTag("Player");
            if (p) player = p.transform;
        }
    }

    void Update()
    {
        if (player == null || currentTargets.Count == 0) return;

        // ?寃?媛쒖닔留뚰겮 ?쇱씤 洹몃━湲?
        for (int i = 0; i < currentTargets.Count; i++)
        {
            // ?쇱씤??紐⑥옄?쇰㈃ ?앹꽦 ????(?덉쟾?μ튂)
            if (i >= activeLines.Count) break;

            if (currentTargets[i] != null)
            {
                DrawPath(activeLines[i], currentTargets[i].position);
            }
        }

        // ?띿뒪???먮Ⅴ???좊땲硫붿씠??
        float offset = Time.time * -textureScrollSpeed;
        foreach (var line in activeLines)
        {
            if (line != null && line.material != null) 
                line.material.mainTextureOffset = new Vector2(offset, 0);
        }
    }

    void DrawPath(LineRenderer line, Vector3 targetPos)
    {
        // ?뚮젅?댁뼱 -> ?寃?寃쎈줈 怨꾩궛
        if (NavMesh.CalculatePath(player.position, targetPos, NavMesh.AllAreas, navMeshPath))
        {
            line.positionCount = navMeshPath.corners.Length;
            for (int j = 0; j < navMeshPath.corners.Length; j++)
            {
                Vector3 pos = navMeshPath.corners[j];
                pos.y += pathHeight;
                line.SetPosition(j, pos);
            }
            line.enabled = true;
        }
        else
        {
            line.enabled = false; // 湲??놁쑝硫??④?
        }
    }

    // ??[?듭떖] ?寃잛쓣 ?щ윭 媛?諛쏆븘??媛곴컖 ?쇱씤???앹꽦??
    public void ShowPath(params Transform[] newTargets)
    {
        // 湲곗〈 ?쇱씤 ??吏?곌린
        HidePath();

        if (newTargets == null || linePrefab == null) return;

        // ?寃??깅줉
        currentTargets.AddRange(newTargets);

        // ?寃?媛쒖닔留뚰겮 ?쇱씤 ?꾨━???앹꽦
        for (int i = 0; i < currentTargets.Count; i++)
        {
            GameObject lineObj = Instantiate(linePrefab, transform); // ?먯떇?쇰줈 ?앹꽦
            LineRenderer lr = lineObj.GetComponent<LineRenderer>();
            if (lr)
            {
                lr.enabled = true;
                activeLines.Add(lr);
            }
        }
    }

    public void HidePath()
    {
        // ?앹꽦?덈뜕 ?쇱씤??紐⑤몢 ??젣
        foreach (var line in activeLines)
        {
            if (line != null) Destroy(line.gameObject);
        }
        activeLines.Clear();
        currentTargets.Clear();
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

public class LobbyEnemy : MonoBehaviour
{
    // ?쒕줎??Destroy?섍굅??爰쇱쭏 ???몄텧??
    void OnDisable()
    {
        // 寃뚯엫??醫낅즺?섎뒗 以묒씠 ?꾨땺 ?뚮쭔 泥댄겕
        if (gameObject.scene.isLoaded && LobbyManager.Instance != null)
        {
            // 泥대젰?????댁꽌 二쎌? 嫄댁? ?뺤씤?섎젮硫?DroneController??HP瑜?泥댄겕?대룄 ??
            // ?ш린?쒕뒗 媛꾨떒?섍쾶 "?щ씪吏硫?二쎌? 寃??쇰줈 泥섎━
            LobbyManager.Instance.OnEnemyKilled();
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: LobbyManager.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using TMPro;
using DG.Tweening;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class LobbyManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static LobbyManager Instance;

    [Header("??UI ?붿냼")]
    public CanvasGroup dialogueGroup;
    public TextMeshProUGUI speakerText;
    public TextMeshProUGUI contentText;
    public Image alertOverlay;

    [Header("???섏뒪??UI")]
    public CanvasGroup questPanelGroup;
    public TextMeshProUGUI questTitleText;
    public TextMeshProUGUI questDescriptionText;

    [Header("???꾪닾 ?ㅼ젙")]
    public GameObject dronePrefab;
    public Transform[] spawnPoints;
    private int totalEnemyCount = 0;
    private int currentDeadEnemy = 0;

    [Header("???섎━踰좎씠???곹샇?묒슜")]
    public GameObject elevatorPanel;
    public string nextSceneName = "HangarScene";

    [Header("???섎━踰좎씠??臾??ㅼ젙")]
    public Transform doorLeft;
    public Transform doorRight;
    public float doorOpenHeight = 4.0f;
    public float doorOpenSpeed = 2.0f;

    [Header("???ㅻ퉬寃뚯씠??& ?몃━嫄?)]
    public GuidePathSystem guideSystem;
    public Transform triggerApproach; // 臾????몃━嫄?
    public Transform triggerBoard;    // ?덉そ ?몃━嫄?
    
    [Tooltip("寃쎌쑀吏 由ъ뒪??)]
    public Transform[] pathWaypoints; 
    private List<Transform> activeWaypoints = new List<Transform>();

    [Header("??????ㅼ젙")]
    [Range(0.01f, 0.2f)] public float textTypingSpeed = 0.05f;

    // ?곹깭 蹂??(?몃? ?묎렐???꾪빐 ?꾨줈?쇳떚 異붽?)
    private bool isDoorReached = false;
    public bool IsDoorReached => isDoorReached; // ???몃━嫄곗뿉???뺤씤??

    private bool isBoarded = false;
    public bool IsBoarded => isBoarded;   
    
    // ?대? ?곹깭 蹂??履쎌뿉 異붽??섏꽭??
    private bool isBranchSelected = false;      // ???몃━嫄곗뿉???뺤씤??

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance == null) Instance = this;
    }

    void Start()
    {
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.alpha = 0;
        if (questPanelGroup) { questPanelGroup.alpha = 0; questPanelGroup.interactable = false; }
        if (alertOverlay) alertOverlay.color = new Color(1, 0, 0, 0); 
        if (elevatorPanel) elevatorPanel.SetActive(false); 

        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Arrival());
    }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // 1. ?꾩갑 & 寃쎈낫
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator Sequence_Arrival()
    {
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "?ш린媛 ?덈뱾???ъ옣遺??, 2.0f));
        if (alertOverlay) alertOverlay.DOFade(0.3f, 0.5f).SetLoops(6, LoopType.Yoyo);
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("?쒖뒪??, "移⑥엯??媛먯?. 諛⑹뼱 ?꾨줈?좎퐳??媛?숉빀?덈떎.", 3.0f));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "?ㅻⅨ 湲몄? ?녿뒗 寃?媛숈븘, ????앸뱾???곕윭?⑤젮???대룞 ?????덇쿋?붾뜲?", 2.5f));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "醫뗭븘. ??遺?댁＜吏.", 2.0f));
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);
        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Combat());
    }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // 2. ?꾪닾
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator Sequence_Combat()
    {
        currentDeadEnemy = 0;
        UpdateQuestUI("?꾩옱 紐⑺몴", "?곷뱾???섑??ъ뒿?덈떎. 紐⑤몢 泥섏튂?섏꽭??");
        if (questPanelGroup) { questPanelGroup.DOFade(1, 0.5f); questPanelGroup.transform.DOPunchScale(Vector3.one * 0.1f, 0.3f); }

        if (dronePrefab && spawnPoints.Length > 0)
        {
            totalEnemyCount = spawnPoints.Length;
            Transform playerTr = GameObject.FindWithTag("Player").transform;
            for (int i = 0; i < spawnPoints.Length; i++)
            {
                GameObject drone = Instantiate(dronePrefab, spawnPoints[i].position, spawnPoints[i].rotation);
                var ai = drone.GetComponent<DroneController>();
                if (ai) { ai.target = playerTr; ai.enabled = true; }
                var tracker = drone.GetComponent<LobbyEnemy>();
                if (tracker == null) tracker = drone.AddComponent<LobbyEnemy>();
            }
        }
        else { totalEnemyCount = 1; OnEnemyKilled(); }

        UpdateQuestUI("?꾪닾 媛쒖떆", $"?곸쓣 ?щ㈇?섏꽭?? ({currentDeadEnemy} / {totalEnemyCount})");
        yield return new WaitUntil(() => currentDeadEnemy >= totalEnemyCount);
        yield return new WaitForSeconds(1.0f);
        StartCoroutine(Sequence_PostCombat());
    }

    public void OnEnemyKilled()
    {
        currentDeadEnemy++;
        UpdateQuestUI("?꾪닾 以?, $"?곸쓣 ?щ㈇?섏꽭?? ({currentDeadEnemy} / {totalEnemyCount})");
        if (questDescriptionText) questDescriptionText.transform.DOPunchScale(Vector3.one * 0.2f, 0.15f);
    }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // 3. ?꾪닾 醫낅즺 & ?대룞 ?좊룄
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator Sequence_PostCombat()
    {
        UpdateQuestUI("?꾪닾 ?꾨즺", "紐⑤뱺 ?곸쓣 泥섏튂?덉뒿?덈떎.");
        isDoorReached = false; 

        yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "醫뗭븘, ???뚭눼??寃?媛숈븘. 吏??蹂묐젰?????댁긽 ?ㅼ? ?딄퀬 ?덉뼱.", 2.0f));
        // ... (以묎컙 ????앸왂 - ?꾩슂??異붽?) ...
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "萸붽? ?좊룄 ?뱁븯???먮굦?댁?留??댁찓 ???놁?.", 1.5f));

        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);
        UpdateQuestUI("?대룞", "?꾨줎???ㅽ렪 ?섎━踰좎씠?곕줈 ?대룞?섏꽭??");

        if (guideSystem)
        {
            activeWaypoints.Clear();
            if (pathWaypoints != null)
            {
                foreach (var wp in pathWaypoints)
                {
                    if (wp != null) { wp.gameObject.SetActive(true); activeWaypoints.Add(wp); }
                }
            }
            UpdateNavigationPath();
        }

        // 臾????꾩갑 ?湲?(誘몃━ ?꾩갑?대룄 LobbyTrigger媛 Stay 媛먯??댁꽌 true濡?諛붽퓭以?
        yield return new WaitUntil(() => isDoorReached == true);

        StartCoroutine(Sequence_AtElevator());
    }

    public void OnWaypointReached(Transform reachedPoint)
    {
        if (activeWaypoints.Contains(reachedPoint))
        {
            activeWaypoints.Remove(reachedPoint);
            UpdateNavigationPath();
        }
    }

    void UpdateNavigationPath()
    {
        if (guideSystem == null) return;
        List<Transform> pathList = new List<Transform>();
        pathList.AddRange(activeWaypoints);
        if (triggerApproach) pathList.Add(triggerApproach);
        guideSystem.ShowPath(pathList.ToArray());
    }

    public void OnReachElevator() 
    { 
        isDoorReached = true; 
        if(guideSystem) guideSystem.HidePath();
    }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // 4. ?섎━踰좎씠???꾩갑 (臾??대┝)
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator Sequence_AtElevator()
    {
        if (doorLeft)
        {
            Collider col = doorLeft.GetComponent<Collider>();
            if (col) col.isTrigger = true; 
            doorLeft.transform.DOLocalMoveY(doorLeft.transform.localPosition.y + doorOpenHeight, doorOpenSpeed).SetEase(Ease.OutQuad);
        }
        if (doorRight) 
        {
            Collider col = doorRight.GetComponent<Collider>();
            if (col) col.isTrigger = true; 
            doorRight.transform.DOLocalMoveY(doorRight.transform.localPosition.y + doorOpenHeight, doorOpenSpeed).SetEase(Ease.OutQuad);
        }

        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "?꾨Т由?遊먮룄 ?뺤긽?곸씤 ?섎━踰좎씠?곕뒗 ?꾨땶 嫄?媛숈???", 2.0f));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "洹몃윭寃? ???곗씠?곕쿋?댁뒪?먮룄 ???섎━踰좎씠?곗뿉 ????뺣낫???놁뼱.", 2.0f));
        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);
        
        UpdateQuestUI("?대룞", "?섎━踰좎씠?곗뿉 ?묒듅?섏꽭??");

        if (guideSystem && triggerBoard) guideSystem.ShowPath(triggerBoard);

        isBoarded = false;
        // ??誘몃━ ?ㅼ뼱媛 ?덉뼱??LobbyTrigger??OnTriggerStay媛 isBoarded瑜?true濡?留뚮뱾?댁쨲
        yield return new WaitUntil(() => isBoarded == true);

        if (guideSystem) guideSystem.HidePath();
        StartCoroutine(Sequence_Inside());
    }

    public void OnEnterElevator() { isBoarded = true; }

    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    // 5. ?묒듅 & ?곹샇?묒슜
    // ??????????????????????????????????????????????
    IEnumerator Sequence_Inside()
    {
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "???섎━踰좎씠?곕룄 寃쎈줈媛 ?쒗븳???덉뼱. 寃⑸궔怨?痢듭쑝濡쒕쭔 ?곌껐?섎뒗 ??빐.", 2.0f));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("異붿삩", "??냸?ㅼ씠 ?쇰????댁뼱??湲몄씤媛.", 2.0f));
        yield return StartCoroutine(PlayDialogue("EGO", "洹몃윺 媛?μ꽦???믪븘. 議곗떖?? 臾댁뼵媛媛 ?곕┫ 湲곕떎由ш퀬 ?덉뼱.", 2.0f));

        if (dialogueGroup) dialogueGroup.DOFade(0, 0.5f);

        UpdateQuestUI("紐⑺몴", "?⑤꼸??議곗옉?섏뿬 痢듭쓣 ?대룞?섏꽭??");

        if (elevatorPanel)
        {
            elevatorPanel.SetActive(true);
            elevatorPanel.transform.DOPunchScale(Vector3.one * 0.2f, 0.5f);
        }
    }

    public void OnInteractElevator()
    {
        if (SceneFader.Instance) SceneFader.Instance.FadeOutAndLoadScene(nextSceneName);
        else UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.LoadScene(nextSceneName);
    }

    IEnumerator PlayDialogue(string speaker, string content, float waitTime)
    {
        dialogueGroup.alpha = 1;
        if (speakerText) speakerText.text = speaker;
        if (contentText)
        {
            contentText.text = "";
            foreach (char c in content)
            {
                contentText.text += c;
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(textTypingSpeed); 
            }
        }
        yield return new WaitForSeconds(waitTime);
    }

    // 媛덈┝湲??몃━嫄?BranchTrigger)媛 ?몄텧?섎뒗 ?⑥닔
    public void OnBranchSelected()
    {
        if (!isBranchSelected)
        {
            isBranchSelected = true;
            Debug.Log(">> [LobbyManager] 媛덈┝湲??좏깮??");
        }
    }

    void UpdateQuestUI(string title, string desc)
    {
        if (questTitleText) questTitleText.text = title;
        if (questDescriptionText) questDescriptionText.text = desc;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: LobbyTrigger.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

public class LobbyTrigger : MonoBehaviour
{
    public enum TriggerType { Approach, Board }
    public TriggerType type;

    private bool isTriggered = false;

    // ?ㅼ뼱媛???媛먯?
    void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
    {
        CheckTrigger(other);
    }

    // ??[異붽??? ?대? ?ㅼ뼱媛?????덉쓣 ?뚮룄 媛먯? (?닿쾶 ?덉뼱??誘몃━ 媛???묐룞??
    void OnTriggerStay(Collider other)
    {
        CheckTrigger(other);
    }

    void CheckTrigger(Collider other)
    {
        // ?대? ?꾨즺???곹깭硫?臾댁떆 (留ㅻ땲?媛 蹂?섎? 珥덇린?뷀븯硫??ㅼ떆 ?묐룞??
        if (isTriggered) 
        {
            // 留ㅻ땲? ?곹깭瑜??뺤씤?댁꽌, 留ㅻ땲?媛 '?꾩쭅 ??諛잛븯??false)'怨??앷컖?섎㈃ ?ㅼ떆 ?좏샇 蹂대깂
            if (LobbyManager.Instance != null)
            {
                if (type == TriggerType.Board && !LobbyManager.Instance.IsBoarded) 
                    isTriggered = false; // ?ㅼ떆 ?묐룞?섎룄濡?由ъ뀑
                else if (type == TriggerType.Approach && !LobbyManager.Instance.IsDoorReached)
                    isTriggered = false;
            }
            
            if(isTriggered) return; 
        }

        if (other.CompareTag("Player"))
        {
            Debug.Log($"[LobbyTrigger] ?뚮젅?댁뼱 媛먯???(Stay/Enter)! ??? {type}");

            if (LobbyManager.Instance != null)
            {
                if (type == TriggerType.Approach)
                {
                    LobbyManager.Instance.OnReachElevator();
                    isTriggered = true;
                }
                else if (type == TriggerType.Board)
                {
                    LobbyManager.Instance.OnEnterElevator();
                    isTriggered = true;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: SceneFader.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using DG.Tweening;

public class SceneFader : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static SceneFader Instance;

    [Header("UI ?곌껐")]
    public CanvasGroup fadeCanvasGroup; // 寃????⑤꼸
    public float fadeDuration = 1.0f;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance == null)
        {
            Instance = this;
            DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject); // ???대룞?대룄 ?뚭눼?섏? ?딆쓬
        }
        else
        {
            // ?대? ?섏씠?붽? ?덈떎硫? 吏湲??ъ뿉 ?덈뒗 嫄?以묐났?대땲 ??젣
            // ?? ??젣?섍린 ?꾩뿉 留뚯빟 吏湲??ъ쓽 ?섏씠?붿뿉留?罹붾쾭?ㅺ? ?곌껐?섏뼱 ?덈떎硫??뺣낫瑜??섍꺼以?(?덉쟾?μ튂)
            if (Instance.fadeCanvasGroup == null && fadeCanvasGroup != null)
            {
                Instance.fadeCanvasGroup = fadeCanvasGroup;
            }
            Destroy(gameObject);
            return;
        }
    }

    private void OnEnable()
    {
        SceneManager.sceneLoaded += OnSceneLoaded;
    }

    private void OnDisable()
    {
        SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnSceneLoaded;
    }

    // 1. 寃뚯엫??泥섏쓬 耳쒖죱????(Start)
    void Start()
    {
        FadeIn();
    }

    // 2. ?ъ씠 ?대룞?덉쓣 ??(OnSceneLoaded)
    private void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
    {
        FadeIn();
    }

    // 怨듯넻: 寃? ?붾㈃ -> ?щ챸?섍쾶 (諛앹븘吏?
    void FadeIn()
    {
        if (fadeCanvasGroup)
        {
            fadeCanvasGroup.alpha = 1; // ?쇰떒 寃寃??쒖옉
            fadeCanvasGroup.blocksRaycasts = true; // ?곗튂 諛⑹?

            fadeCanvasGroup.DOFade(0, fadeDuration)
                .SetEase(Ease.InOutQuad)
                .OnComplete(() => {
                    fadeCanvasGroup.blocksRaycasts = false; // ?앸굹硫?議곗옉 ?덉슜
                });
        }
    }

    // ?몃? ?몄텧?? ?щ챸 -> 寃? ?붾㈃ (?대몢?뚯쭚) ???대룞
    public void FadeOutAndLoadScene(string sceneName)
    {
        if (fadeCanvasGroup)
        {
            fadeCanvasGroup.blocksRaycasts = true;
            fadeCanvasGroup.DOFade(1, fadeDuration)
                .SetEase(Ease.InOutQuad)
                .OnComplete(() => SceneManager.LoadScene(sceneName));
        }
        else
        {
            SceneManager.LoadScene(sceneName);
        }
    }
}

// File: WaypointTrigger.cs

using UnityEngine;

public class WaypointTrigger : MonoBehaviour
{
    private bool isReached = false;

    void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
    {
        if (isReached) return;

        if (other.CompareTag("Player"))
        {
            isReached = true;
            
            // 留ㅻ땲??먭쾶 "???꾩갑?덉뼱!" ?쇨퀬 ?뚮┝
            if (LobbyManager.Instance != null)
            {
                LobbyManager.Instance.OnWaypointReached(this.transform);
            }

            // ?먭린 ?먯떊? ?꾧린 (?щ씪吏?
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

public class TitleManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("?대룞?????대쫫")]
    public string nextSceneName = "LobbyScene"; // ?뱀? "TutorialScene"

    // ?쒖옉 踰꾪듉???곌껐???⑥닔
    public void OnStartButtonClick()
    {
        // ???섏씠?붽? ?덉쑝硫??섏씠???④낵? ?④퍡 ?대룞
        if (SceneFader.Instance != null)
        {
            SceneFader.Instance.FadeOutAndLoadScene(nextSceneName);
        }
        else
        {
            // ?놁쑝硫?洹몃깷 ?대룞 (鍮꾩긽??
            SceneManager.LoadScene(nextSceneName);
        }
    }
    
    // (?좏깮) 醫낅즺 踰꾪듉
    public void OnExitButtonClick()
    {
        Debug.Log("寃뚯엫 醫낅즺");
        Application.Quit();
    }
}

// File: UI_UltimateGauge.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class UI_UltimateGauge : MonoBehaviour
{
    // ========================= 변수 헤더(한글 설명) =========================
    [Header("① 채움 이미지 | 게이지 바(필수)")]
    [Tooltip("Image Type=Filled 인 채움 이미지")]
    public Image fill;

    [Header("② 준비 이펙트 | 100% 도달 시 켜질 FX(선택)")]
    [Tooltip("게이지가 가득 찰 때 활성화할 오브젝트")]
    public GameObject readyFx;

    [Header("③ 보간 속도 | 채움량 부드럽게 이동 속도")]
    [Tooltip("값이 높을수록 빠르게 따라간다")]
    public float lerpSpeed = 6f;

    // ========================= 내부 상태 =========================
    static UI_UltimateGauge _inst;   // 정적 접근(컨트롤러에서 호출)
    float _target;                   // 목표 채움 비율 0~1
    float _current;                  // 현재 채움 비율 0~1
    bool _ready;

    void Awake()
    {
        _inst = this;
        SetInternal(0f);
        SetReadyInternal(false);
    }

    void Update()
    {
        _current = Mathf.Lerp(_current, _target, Time.deltaTime * lerpSpeed);
        if (fill) fill.fillAmount = _current;
    }

    // ============ 공개 정적 API(컨트롤러/시스템에서 호출) ============
    public static void UpdateValue(float ratio)
    {
        if (_inst == null) return;
        _inst.SetInternal(Mathf.Clamp01(ratio));
    }

    public static void SetReady(bool ready)
    {
        if (_inst == null) return;
        _inst.SetReadyInternal(ready);
    }

    // ========================= 내부 구현 =========================
    void SetInternal(float ratio01)
    {
        _target = ratio01;
        if (_target < 1f && readyFx) readyFx.SetActive(false);
    }

    void SetReadyInternal(bool ready)
    {
        _ready = ready;
        if (readyFx) readyFx.SetActive(_ready);
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

// [UI 어댑터] - PlayerUltimateController의 게이지를 UI로 전달
public class UltimateGaugeUIAdapter : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("소스 컨트롤러")]
    public PlayerUltimateController source;

    void Update()
    {
        if (source == null) return;
        float ratio = source.Gauge / Mathf.Max(1f, source.gaugeMax);
        UI_UltimateGauge.UpdateValue(ratio);
        UI_UltimateGauge.SetReady(ratio >= 1f - 0.0001f);
    }
}

// File: PlayerUltimateController.cs

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Playables;
using Cinemachine;
using System;
using System.Collections;

public class PlayerUltimateController : MonoBehaviour
{
    // ========================= 蹂???ㅻ뜑(?쒓? ?ㅻ챸) =========================
    [Header("??寃뚯씠吏 ?ㅼ젙 | 沅곴레湲?諛쒕룞源뚯? ?꾩슂??珥앸웾怨??섍툒??)]
    [Tooltip("?섏씠利?肄붿뼱 理쒕?移?%)")]
    public float gaugeMax = 100f;
    [Tooltip("??媛?怨듦꺽 紐낆쨷 ??寃뚯씠吏 利앷???%)")]
    public float gaugePerH= 3f;
    [Tooltip("?⑤쭅 ?깃났 ??寃뚯씠吏 利앷???%)")]
    public float gaugePerParry =6f;
    [Tooltip("?쇳럺???뚰뵾 ?깃났 ??寃뚯씠吏 利앷???%)")]
    public float gaugePerPerfectDodge = 16;

    [Header("??諛쒕룞 議곌굔 | 諛쒕룞 ??泥댄겕??議곌굔??(?대룞, 寃쎌쭅, 怨듭쨷 ??")]
    [Tooltip("?먰봽 以?諛쒕룞 ?덉슜 ?щ?")]
    public bool allowInAir = false;
    [Tooltip("?쇨꺽 寃쎌쭅 以?諛쒕룞 湲덉?")]
    public bool blockWhenStaggered = true;

    [Header("???곗텧/?곹깭 | ?곗텧 以?臾댁쟻, 議곗옉 ?좉툑, ?쎌삩/移대찓??泥섎━")]
    [Tooltip("?곗텧 以?臾댁쟻 ?곸슜 ?щ?")]
    public bool invulnerableDuringCinematic = true;
    [Tooltip("?곗텧 以??뚮젅?댁뼱 議곗옉 ?좉툑")]
    public bool lockInputDuringCinematic = true;
    [Tooltip("?곗텧 醫낅즺 ???쎌삩/移대찓?쇰? ?먯쐞移??쒗궗吏 ?щ?")]
    public bool restoreCameraAndLockOn = true;

    [Header("???곕?吏 ?ㅼ젙 | 蹂댁뒪 怨좎젙 ?곕?吏 諛?蹂댁젙移?)]
    [Tooltip("?쇰땲???쇱꽟 怨좎젙 ?곕?吏")]
    public int finisherFixedDamage = 1200;
    [Tooltip("?ㅻ떒 李멸꺽 1?덊듃??怨좎젙 ?곕?吏")]
    public int multihitFixedDamage = 120;

    [Header("????꾨씪???덊띁?곗뒪 | 而룹뵮 諛?移대찓??諛붿씤??)]
    [Tooltip("沅곴레湲?而룹뵮??PlayableDirector")]
    public PlayableDirector director;
    [Tooltip("而룹뵮 ?숈븞 ?ъ슜???쒕꽕癒몄떊 移대찓?쇰뱾(?쒖꽌?濡?釉붾젋??")]
    public CinemachineVirtualCamera[] vCams;

    [Header("???ъ뒪???꾩껜?댄럺??| ?묐갚, 鍮꾨꽕?? ?붾㈃洹좎뿴 ???좉?")]
    [Tooltip("??ㅽ겕由??묐갚 ?≪엯 ?곗씠??而⑦듃濡ㅻ윭")]
    public UltimateScreenFX screenFX;
    [Tooltip("?쇰땲??吏곹썑 ?붾㈃ 洹좎뿴 ?댄럺??)]
    public GameObject glassCrackPrefab;

    [Header("?????대깽??| ?몃? ?쒖뒪?쒓낵???곕룞 ?대깽??)]
    public Action OnUltimateStarted;
    public Action OnUltimateEnded;

    // ========================= ?대? ?곹깭 =========================
    public float Gauge { get; private set; }
    bool _isCinematic;

    IInputBlocker _input;                // ?꾨줈?앺듃 怨듭슜 ?낅젰李⑤떒 ?명꽣?섏씠??
    ILockOnController _lockOn;           // ?쎌삩 而⑦듃濡ㅻ윭
    IInvulnerabilityToggle _invul;       // 臾댁쟻 ?좉?(?대뙌???명꽣?섏씠??
    ICombatStateReader _combat;          // 寃쎌쭅/怨듦꺽以??щ? ???곹깭 議고쉶

    void Awake()
    {
        _input  = GetComponent<IInputBlocker>();
        _lockOn = GetComponent<ILockOnController>();
        _invul  = GetComponent<IInvulnerabilityToggle>();
        _combat = GetComponent<ICombatStateReader>();
    }

    // 寃뚯씠吏 ?섍툒(怨듦꺽/?⑤쭅/?쇳럺?명쉶?쇱뿉???몄텧)
    public void AddGauge(float amount)
    {
        if (_isCinematic) return;
        Gauge = Mathf.Clamp(Gauge + amount, 0f, gaugeMax);
        // UI 媛깆떊 ?붿껌 (?섏씠利?肄붿뼱 ?뚯쟾/?됱긽 ?④퀎)
        UI_UltimateGauge.UpdateValue(Gauge / gaugeMax);
    }

    // R ?낅젰?먯꽌 ?몄텧
    public bool TryActivate()
    {
        if (_isCinematic) return false;
        if (Gauge < gaugeMax) return false;
        if (blockWhenStaggered && _combat != null && _combat.IsStaggered()) return false;
        if (!allowInAir && _combat != null && _combat.IsInAir()) return false;

        StartCoroutine(Co_Cinematic());
        return true;
    }

    IEnumerator Co_Cinematic()
    {
        _isCinematic = true;
        Gauge = 0f; // 1?뚯슜 ?뚮え
        UI_UltimateGauge.UpdateValue(0);

        // ?낅젰/臾댁쟻/移대찓??沅뚰븳
        if (lockInputDuringCinematic) _input?.BlockAll(true);
        if (invulnerableDuringCinematic) _invul?.SetInvulnerable(true);
        _lockOn?.GiveCameraControlToTimeline(true);

        OnUltimateStarted?.Invoke();

        // 0) ?꾨━濡? 肄붿뼱 ??＜ ?쒖옉(?붾㈃ ?묐갚 ?≪엯 ?댄럺??0??)
        screenFX?.PlayChargeIn();

        // 1) ??꾨씪???뚮젅??(?좊땲/移대찓???ㅻ뵒??VFX ?듯빀)
        if (director != null)
        {
            director.time = 0;
            director.Evaluate();
            director.Play();
        }

        // ??꾨씪??Signal?먯꽌 ?꾨옒 ?낆쓣 ?몄텧?섎룄濡?諛곗꽑:
        //  - Signal ?쏮ultiHit????OnMultiHit()
        //  - Signal ?쏤inisher????OnFinisher()
        //  - Signal ?쏞utsceneEnd????(?꾨옒 醫낅즺 泥섎━)

        // ?湲? ??꾨씪??醫낅즺源뚯?
        while (director != null && director.state == PlayState.Playing)
            yield return null;

        // 醫낅즺 泥섎━
        screenFX?.PlayChargeOut();
        if (restoreCameraAndLockOn) _lockOn?.GiveCameraControlToTimeline(false);
        if (invulnerableDuringCinematic) _invul?.SetInvulnerable(false);
        if (lockInputDuringCinematic) _input?.BlockAll(false);

        OnUltimateEnded?.Invoke();
        _isCinematic = false;
    }

    // --- ??꾨씪??Signal ??---
    // ?ㅻ떒李멸꺽 ??대컢?ㅼ뿉???대깽?몃줈 ?몄텧
    public void OnMultiHit()
    {
        ApplyBurstDamage(multihitFixedDamage);
        screenFX?.PulseMinor(); // ?쏀븳 ?붾㈃ ?꾩뒪
    }

    // ?쇰땲???쇱꽟 ??대컢?먯꽌 ?대깽?몃줈 ?몄텧
    public void OnFinisher()
    {
        ApplyBurstDamage(finisherFixedDamage);
        if (glassCrackPrefab) Instantiate(glassCrackPrefab, Vector3.zero, Quaternion.identity);
        screenFX?.PulseMajor(); // 媛뺥븳 ?붾㈃ ?꾩뒪
    }

    void ApplyBurstDamage(int damage)
    {
        // ?꾩옱 ?쎌삩 ??곸뿉 ?곗꽑 ?곸슜
        var target = _lockOn?.GetCurrentTarget();
        if (target != null && target.TryGetComponent<IUltimateTarget>(out var ult))
            ult.ApplyUltimateDamage(damage);
        else
        {
            // 蹂댁뒪 ?쒓렇 ?쒖튂(?쎌삩???놁쓣 ?뚯쓽 ?대갚)
            var boss = GameObject.FindWithTag("Boss");
            if (boss != null && boss.TryGetComponent<IUltimateTarget>(out var ult2))
                ult2.ApplyUltimateDamage(damage);
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: ScreenFXSignalHooks.cs
// ==========================================

using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

public class ScreenFXSignalHooks : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] UltimateScreenFX fx;
    [SerializeField] float defaultPulseStrength = 1f;
    [SerializeField] float defaultPulseDuration = 0.15f;

    public void Pulse()
    {
        if (fx != null) fx.Flash(defaultPulseStrength, defaultPulseDuration);
    }

    public void SetStrength(float v)
    {
        if (fx != null) fx.SetStrength(v);
    }

    public void FadeOutGray(float dur)
    {
        if (fx == null) return;
        StartCoroutine(FadeOut(dur));
    }

    IEnumerator FadeOut(float dur)
    {
        float t = 0f;
        while (t < dur)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            fx.SetStrength(Mathf.Lerp(1f, 0f, t / dur));
            yield return null;
        }
        fx.SetStrength(0f);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: SlashSequenceBehaviour.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Playables;

public class SlashSequenceBehaviour : PlayableBehaviour
{
    public UltimateSlashBurstSpawner spawner;
    public int total = 15;
    public float interval = 0.05f;
    public int patternSeed = 13579;
    public float slashLife = 0.7f;

    int emitted;
    float nextTime;

    public override void OnGraphStart(Playable playable)
    {
        emitted = 0;
        nextTime = 0f;
    }

    public override void ProcessFrame(Playable playable, FrameData info, object playerData)
    {
        if (spawner == null || emitted >= total) return;

        // playable.GetTime()는 클립 시작 기준 시간(초)
        float t = (float)playable.GetTime();

        while (emitted < total && t >= nextTime)
        {
            spawner.EmitOneSlash(emitted, total, patternSeed, slashLife);
            emitted++;
            nextTime += interval;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: SlashSequenceClip.cs
// ==========================================

// SlashSequenceClip.cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Playables;

[System.Serializable]
public class SlashSequenceClip : PlayableAsset
{
    public ExposedReference<UltimateSlashBurstSpawner> spawner;

    [Min(1)] public int total = 15;
    [Min(0f)] public float interval = 0.05f;  // 각 타 사이 간격(초)
    public int patternSeed = 13579;
    public float slashLife = 0.7f;

    // 타임라인에 올렸을 때 클립 길이를 자동으로 보정하면 편함
    public override double duration => Mathf.Max(0.0001f, total * interval);

    public override Playable CreatePlayable(PlayableGraph graph, GameObject owner)
    {
        var playable = ScriptPlayable<SlashSequenceBehaviour>.Create(graph);
        var bhv = playable.GetBehaviour();
        bhv.spawner = spawner.Resolve(graph.GetResolver());
        bhv.total = total;
        bhv.interval = interval;
        bhv.patternSeed = patternSeed;
        bhv.slashLife = slashLife;
        return playable;
    }
}

// SlashSequenceBehaviour.cs

// ==========================================
// File: TimeScaleController.cs
// ==========================================

using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// ?꾩뿭 ??꾩뒪耳??愿由??щ줈紐??꾨━利?蹂듭썝). PlayerDash ?깆뿉???깃??ㅼ쑝濡??묎렐.
/// </summary>
public sealed class TimeScaleController : MonoBehaviour
{
    // ===================== [議곗젅媛??ㅻ뜑] =====================
    [Header("??꾩뒪耳??湲곕낯 蹂댁젙")]
    [Tooltip("湲곗? fixedDeltaTime(珥?. ??꾩뒪耳??蹂寃???蹂댁젙 湲곗?. 蹂댄넻 0.02")]
    [SerializeField] float baseFixedDeltaTime = 0.02f;

    [Header("?щ줈紐?湲곕낯媛?)]
    [Tooltip("湲곕낯 ?щ줈紐?諛곗쑉(0.1~1.0)")]
    [Range(0.01f, 1f)] [SerializeField] float defaultSlowScale = 0.2f;
    [Tooltip("湲곕낯 ?щ줈紐?吏?띿떆媛?珥?")]
    [SerializeField] float defaultSlowDuration = 0.3f;
    [Tooltip("?щ줈紐?吏꾩엯 釉붾젋???쒓컙(珥?")]
    [SerializeField] float defaultBlendIn = 0.06f;
    [Tooltip("?щ줈紐?蹂듭썝 釉붾젋???쒓컙(珥?")]
    [SerializeField] float defaultBlendOut = 0.12f;

    [Header("?꾨━利??꾨젅??)]
    [Tooltip("?꾨━利??꾨젅??湲곕낯 吏??珥?. ?꾩＜ 吏㏐쾶 ?ъ슜 沅뚯옣")]
    [SerializeField] float defaultFreezeDuration = 0.05f;

    // ===================== [?곹깭媛? =====================
    [Tooltip("?꾩옱 ?곸슜 以???꾩뒪耳???쎄린?꾩슜)")]
    public float CurrentScale { get; private set; } = 1f;

    public static TimeScaleController Instance { get; private set; }

    Coroutine _lerpCo;
    float _restoreAtUnscaled = 0f;
    bool _pendingAutoRestore = false;

    void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;
        DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);
        baseFixedDeltaTime = Mathf.Max(0.001f, baseFixedDeltaTime);
        ApplyScale(1f);
    }

    void Update()
    {
        if (_pendingAutoRestore && Time.unscaledTime >= _restoreAtUnscaled)
        {
            _pendingAutoRestore = false;
            _restoreAtUnscaled = 0f;
            // 遺?쒕읇寃??먮났
            Restore(defaultBlendOut);
        }
    }

    // ===================== [?몃? API] =====================

    /// <summary>
    /// PlayerDash ?명솚?? 吏??諛곗쑉濡??щ줈紐?吏꾩엯 ??duration ???먮룞 蹂듭썝.
    /// </summary>
    public void SetSlowMotion(float toScale, float duration)
    {
        toScale = Mathf.Clamp(toScale, 0.01f, 1f);
        // 吏꾩엯
        SetTimeScale(toScale, defaultBlendIn);
        // ?덉빟 蹂듭썝
        _pendingAutoRestore = true;
        _restoreAtUnscaled = Time.unscaledTime + Mathf.Max(0f, duration);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 湲곕낯媛믪쑝濡??щ줈紐??ㅽ뻾.
    /// </summary>
    public void SlowMoDefault()
        => SetSlowMotion(defaultSlowScale, defaultSlowDuration);

    /// <summary>
    /// ?꾨━利??꾨젅???꾩쟾 ?뺤? ???먮룞 蹂듭썝).
    /// </summary>
    public void FreezeFrame(float duration = -1f)
    {
        if (duration <= 0f) duration = defaultFreezeDuration;
        // 利됱떆 0?쇰줈
        if (_lerpCo != null) StopCoroutine(_lerpCo);
        ApplyScale(0f);
        _pendingAutoRestore = true;
        _restoreAtUnscaled = Time.unscaledTime + duration;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 利됱떆 ?먮뒗 釉붾젋?쒕줈 ?먮났.
    /// </summary>
    public void ResetTimeScale(float blendOut = -1f)
    {
        if (blendOut < 0f) blendOut = defaultBlendOut;
        _pendingAutoRestore = false;
        _restoreAtUnscaled = 0f;
        Restore(blendOut);
    }

    // ===================== [湲곗〈 ?명솚 API] =====================

    /// <summary>
    /// ?먰븯??諛곗쑉濡?釉붾젋??吏꾩엯.
    /// </summary>
    public void SetTimeScale(float toScale, float blendDuration = 0.1f)
    {
        if (_lerpCo != null) StopCoroutine(_lerpCo);
        _lerpCo = StartCoroutine(LerpTimeScale(toScale, blendDuration));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 1.0?쇰줈 釉붾젋??蹂듭썝.
    /// </summary>
    public void Restore(float blendDuration = 0.2f)
    {
        if (_lerpCo != null) StopCoroutine(_lerpCo);
        _lerpCo = StartCoroutine(LerpTimeScale(1f, blendDuration));
    }

    // ===================== [?대? 援ы쁽] =====================

    IEnumerator LerpTimeScale(float target, float dur)
    {
        float start = Time.timeScale;
        float t = 0f;
        dur = Mathf.Max(0f, dur);
        if (dur == 0f)
        {
            ApplyScale(target);
            _lerpCo = null;
            yield break;
        }

        while (t < dur)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            float k = Mathf.Clamp01(t / dur);
            ApplyScale(Mathf.Lerp(start, target, k));
            yield return null;
        }
        ApplyScale(target);
        _lerpCo = null;
    }

    void ApplyScale(float scale)
    {
        CurrentScale = scale;
        Time.timeScale = scale;
        Time.fixedDeltaTime = baseFixedDeltaTime * Mathf.Max(scale, 0.0001f);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: UltimateFinisherHooks.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

public class UltimateFinisherHooks : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Anim & FX")]
    [SerializeField] private Animator playerAnimator;
    [SerializeField] private string finisherTrigger = "Ultimate_Finish";
    [SerializeField] private GameObject explodePrefab;
    [SerializeField] private Transform explodePoint;

    [Header("Optional: Time & Screen FX")]
    [SerializeField] private TimeScaleController timeCtrl;     // 없어도 동작
    [SerializeField] private ScreenFXSignalHooks screenFX;     // 없어도 동작

    // 타임라인 Signal에서 이 메서드만 호출하면 됨
    public void DoFinisher()
    {
        if (playerAnimator && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(finisherTrigger))
            playerAnimator.SetTrigger(finisherTrigger);

        if (explodePrefab && explodePoint)
            Instantiate(explodePrefab, explodePoint.position, explodePoint.rotation);

        if (screenFX) screenFX.FadeOutGray(0.3f);
        if (timeCtrl) timeCtrl.Restore(0.3f);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: UltimateScreenFX.cs
// ==========================================

// Assets/Scripts/Ultimate/UltimateScreenFX.cs
using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

[ExecuteAlways]
public class UltimateScreenFX : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Assign the Quad's Renderer")]
    [SerializeField] private Renderer targetRenderer;
    [SerializeField] private string propStrength = "_Strength"; // ?곗씠???꾨줈?쇳떚紐?

    [Header("Curves & Timings")]
    [SerializeField] private AnimationCurve ease = AnimationCurve.EaseInOut(0, 0, 1, 1);

    [Header("Pulse Settings")]
    [SerializeField] private float pulseMinorStrength = 0.25f;
    [SerializeField] private float pulseMajorStrength = 0.6f;
    [SerializeField] private float pulseDuration = 0.25f;

    [Header("Charge Settings")]
    [SerializeField] private float chargeMaxStrength = 0.5f;
    [SerializeField] private float chargeInTime = 0.35f;
    [SerializeField] private float chargeOutTime = 0.25f;

    MaterialPropertyBlock mpb;
    Coroutine running;          // ?꾩옱 ?ㅽ뻾 以묒씤 ?몄쐢/?꾩뒪
    float strength;             // ?꾩옱 媛뺣룄(諛깊궧 ?꾨뱶)

    void Awake()
    {
        if (!targetRenderer) targetRenderer = GetComponentInChildren<Renderer>(true);
        if (mpb == null) mpb = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
        ApplyStrength(0f);
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // ?몃? API (PlayerUltimateController媛 ?몄텧)
    // ---------------------------

    public void PlayChargeIn()
    {
        // 0 -> chargeMaxStrength濡?遺?쒕읇寃?
        StartRoutine(TweenStrength(chargeMaxStrength, chargeInTime));
    }

    public void PlayChargeOut()
    {
        // ?꾩옱媛?-> 0?쇰줈 遺?쒕읇寃?
        StartRoutine(TweenStrength(0f, chargeOutTime));
    }

    public void PulseMinor()
    {
        StartRoutine(Pulse(pulseMinorStrength, pulseDuration));
    }

    public void PulseMajor()
    {
        StartRoutine(Pulse(pulseMajorStrength, pulseDuration));
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // ?명솚??湲곗〈???곕뜕 肄붾뱶 ???
    // ---------------------------

    public void SetStrength(float v)        // 利됱떆 ?멸린 ?ㅼ젙
    {
        ApplyStrength(Mathf.Clamp01(v));
    }

    public void Flash(float strength = 1f, float duration = 0.15f)   // 吏㏐쾶 踰덉찉
    {
        StartRoutine(Pulse(Mathf.Clamp01(strength), Mathf.Max(0.01f, duration)));
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // ?대? 援ы쁽
    // ---------------------------

    void StartRoutine(IEnumerator co)
    {
        if (running != null) StopCoroutine(running);
        running = StartCoroutine(co);
    }

    IEnumerator TweenStrength(float target, float time)
    {
        float from = strength;
        float t = 0f;
        float dur = Mathf.Max(0.01f, time);

        while (t < dur)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            float x = Mathf.Clamp01(t / dur);
            ApplyStrength(Mathf.Lerp(from, target, ease.Evaluate(x)));
            yield return null;
        }
        ApplyStrength(target);
        running = null;
    }

    IEnumerator Pulse(float peak, float dur)
    {
        // up
        float from = strength;
        float half = Mathf.Max(0.01f, dur * 0.5f);
        float t = 0f;
        while (t < half)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            float x = Mathf.Clamp01(t / half);
            ApplyStrength(Mathf.Lerp(from, peak, ease.Evaluate(x)));
            yield return null;
        }

        // down (peak -> 0)
        t = 0f;
        while (t < half)
        {
            t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            float x = Mathf.Clamp01(t / half);
            ApplyStrength(Mathf.Lerp(peak, 0f, ease.Evaluate(x)));
            yield return null;
        }

        ApplyStrength(0f);
        running = null;
    }

    void ApplyStrength(float v)
    {
        strength = v;
        if (!targetRenderer) return;

        if (mpb == null) mpb = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
        targetRenderer.GetPropertyBlock(mpb);
        mpb.SetFloat(propStrength, strength);
        targetRenderer.SetPropertyBlock(mpb);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: UltimateSlashBurstSpawner.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
using System;

public class UltimateSlashBurstSpawner : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Anchors")]
    [SerializeField] Transform player;     // 플레이어 루트
    [SerializeField] Transform spawnRoot;  // Player/UltimateSpawnRoot

    [Header("Prefab & Look")]
    [SerializeField] GameObject slashPrefab;
    [SerializeField] float forwardOffset = 3.0f;
    [SerializeField] float heightOffset = 1.4f;
    [SerializeField] float length = 6.0f;
    [SerializeField] float thickness = 0.08f;
    [SerializeField] float coneAngle = 25f;

    [Header("Material props (optional)")]
    [SerializeField] string propIntensity = "_Intensity";
    [SerializeField] string propScroll = "_ScrollSpeed";
    [SerializeField] string propAlpha = "_Alpha";
    [SerializeField] float baseIntensity = 1.2f;
    [SerializeField] float baseScroll = 2.0f;

    const float Golden = 137.507764f; // 분포용

    void EnsureSpawnRoot()
    {
        if (spawnRoot) return;
        var go = new GameObject("UltimateSpawnRoot");
        spawnRoot = go.transform;
        spawnRoot.SetParent(player, false);
        spawnRoot.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, heightOffset, forwardOffset);
        spawnRoot.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
    }

    // ⬇️ “한 줄”만 생성 (index에 따라 항상 같은 방향/모양)
    public void EmitOneSlash(int index, int totalCount, int patternSeed, float life = 0.7f)
    {
        if (!player) player = transform;
        EnsureSpawnRoot();

        // 고정 시드 + 인덱스로 안정적인 난수 시퀀스
        var rng = new System.Random(patternSeed + index * 9973);

        // 플레이어 정면 기준 회전
        Quaternion faceFwd = Quaternion.LookRotation(player.forward, Vector3.up);

        // 분포: 골든앵글 기반 + 약간의 지터
        float yaw = index * Golden + (float)(rng.NextDouble() * 10f - 5f);
        float pitch = (float)((rng.NextDouble() * 2 - 1) * coneAngle);

        Quaternion rot = faceFwd
                       * Quaternion.AngleAxis(yaw, Vector3.up)
                       * Quaternion.AngleAxis(pitch, Vector3.right);

        Vector3 pos = spawnRoot.position;

        var go = Instantiate(slashPrefab, pos, rot);
        go.transform.localScale = new Vector3(thickness, thickness, length);

        var r = go.GetComponentInChildren<Renderer>();
        if (r)
        {
            var mpb = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            r.GetPropertyBlock(mpb);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propIntensity)) mpb.SetFloat(propIntensity, baseIntensity);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propScroll)) mpb.SetFloat(propScroll, baseScroll);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propAlpha)) mpb.SetFloat(propAlpha, 1f);
            r.SetPropertyBlock(mpb);
        }

        Destroy(go, life);
    }
}
