// ?뚯씪紐? UltimateTargetSimple.cs
// ??븷: IUltimateTarget??湲곕낯 援ы쁽(蹂댁뒪??諛붾줈 遺李?媛??
// ?뱀쭠: '怨좎젙 ?곕?吏' ?낅젰??諛쏆븘 諛⑹뼱/釉뚮젅?댄겕 蹂댁젙 ??泥대젰 媛먯냼 ??VFX/SFX/?덊듃由ъ븸?섍퉴吏 泥섎━
//       ?몃???湲곗〈 Health/Damage ?쒖뒪?쒖씠 ?덉쑝硫? ?꾨옒 UnityEvent濡?釉뚮┸吏???곕룞 媛??

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;

[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public class UltimateTargetSimple : MonoBehaviour, IUltimateTarget
{
    // ================================ 蹂???ㅻ뜑(?쒓? ?ㅻ챸) ================================
    [Header("??泥대젰 ?ㅼ젙 | 理쒕?/?꾩옱 泥대젰 諛??щ쭩 泥섎━")]
    [Tooltip("理쒕? 泥대젰")]
    public int maxHP = 10000;
    [Tooltip("?꾩옱 泥대젰(?고???以?媛깆떊)")]
    public int currentHP = 10000;
    [Tooltip("泥대젰??0 ?댄븯媛 ?섎㈃ ?뚭눼?좎? ?щ?")]
    public bool destroyOnDeath = false;

    [Header("??諛⑹뼱/蹂댁젙 | 怨좎젙?곕?吏?????諛⑹뼱 媛?釉뚮젅?댄겕 蹂댁젙")]
    [Tooltip("諛⑹뼱??怨좎젙 ?곕?吏?먯꽌 媛먯궛). ignoreDefense媛 false???뚮쭔 ?곸슜")]
    public int defense = 200;
    [Tooltip("諛⑹뼱 臾댁떆(怨좎젙 ?곕?吏 洹몃?濡??곸슜)")]
    public bool ignoreDefense = true;
    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕(臾대젰?? ?곹깭 ?щ? - 釉뚮젅?댄겕 以묒씠硫?諛곗쑉 蹂대꼫???곸슜")]
    public bool isBreak = false;
    [Tooltip("釉뚮젅?댄겕 ?곹깭?????곕?吏 諛곗쑉(?? 1.5 = 50% 異붽??쇳빐)")]
    [Min(1f)] public float breakBonusMultiplier = 1.5f;

    [Header("???덊듃 由ъ븸??| ?됰갚/寃쎌쭅/?덊듃?ㅽ넲 ??媛꾨떒 ?듭뀡)")]
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ???쏀븳 寃쎌쭅/由ъ븸?섏쓣 以?由ъ??쒕컮???놁쑝硫?臾댁떆)")]
    public Rigidbody rigidbodyForReaction;
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ??諛爾먮궪 ???꾨갑 湲곗?). 0?대㈃ ?곸슜 ????)]
    public float knockbackForce = 3f;
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ???쏀븳 HitStop(珥?. 0?대㈃ ?곸슜 ????)]
    public float hitStop = 0.03f;

    [Header("???곗텧 | ?쇨꺽 VFX/SFX 諛??ㅽ룿 ?꾩튂")]
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 ???ㅽ룿??VFX ?꾨━???놁쑝硫?誘몄궗??")]
    public GameObject hitVFXPrefab;
    [Tooltip("VFX ?ㅽ룿 湲곗? ?몃옖?ㅽ뤌(?놁쑝硫?this.transform ?ъ슜)")]
    public Transform vfxSpawnPoint;
    [Tooltip("?덊듃 SFX(?놁쑝硫?誘몄궗??")]
    public AudioClip hitSFX;
    [Tooltip("?ㅻ뵒???뚯뒪(?놁쑝硫??먮룞 ?앹꽦)")]
    public AudioSource audioSource;

    [Header("???대깽??| ?몃? ?쒖뒪??湲곗〈 Health ??怨쇱쓽 釉뚮┸吏")]
    [Tooltip("沅곴레湲??쇨꺽 ???곸슜 ?곕?吏 int) ?몄텧 - 湲곗〈 Health濡??쇱슦?낇븷 ???ъ슜")]
    public UnityEvent<int> OnUltimateDamageApplied;
    [Tooltip("?щ쭩 ???몄텧 - 蹂댁뒪 ?섏씠利??곗텧 ?몃━嫄??곌껐")]
    public UnityEvent OnDied;

    // ================================ ?대? ?곹깭/?좏떥 ================================
    bool _isDead;
    float _hitStopRemain;

    void Reset()
    {
        // 而댄룷?뚰듃 異붽? ??湲곕낯媛?珥덇린??
        currentHP = maxHP;
        if (!audioSource) audioSource = GetComponent<AudioSource>();
    }

    void Awake()
    {
        if (!audioSource)
        {
            audioSource = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
            audioSource.playOnAwake = false;
            audioSource.spatialBlend = 1f; // 3D
        }
        if (!vfxSpawnPoint) vfxSpawnPoint = transform;
        currentHP = Mathf.Clamp(currentHP, 0, maxHP);
    }

    void Update()
    {
        // 媛꾨떒???덊듃?ㅽ넲 泥섎━(?꾨줈?뺤뀡?먯꽑 湲濡쒕쾶 留ㅻ땲?/??꾩뒪耳???泥?沅뚯옣)
        if (_hitStopRemain > 0f)
        {
            _hitStopRemain -= Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            // ?꾩슂 ??Animator.speed, AI ?낅뜲?댄듃, ?뚰떚???깆쓣 媛쒕퀎 ?쇱떆?뺤??섎뒗 諛⑹떇?쇰줈 ?뺤옣 媛??
        }
    }

    // ================================ ?듭떖 濡쒖쭅 ================================
    public void ApplyUltimateDamage(int fixedDamage)
    {
        if (_isDead) return;

        // 1) 理쒖쥌 ?곕?吏 怨꾩궛
        int dmg = fixedDamage;
        if (!ignoreDefense)
            dmg = Mathf.Max(1, fixedDamage - Mathf.Max(0, defense));
        if (isBreak && breakBonusMultiplier > 1f)
            dmg = Mathf.RoundToInt(dmg * breakBonusMultiplier);

        // 2) ?몃? ?쒖뒪??釉뚮┸吏(?덈떎硫?癒쇱? ?뚮┝)
        //    - 湲곗〈 Health 而댄룷?뚰듃媛 ???대깽?몃? 諛쏆븘 ?대? 洹쒖튃?濡?泥섎━?섎룄濡??쇱슦??媛??
        OnUltimateDamageApplied?.Invoke(dmg);

        // 3) ?대? HP 泥섎━(?몃?媛 ?녿뒗 寃쎌슦 ?鍮?
        ApplyLocalDamage(dmg);

        // 4) ?곗텧: VFX/SFX/由ъ븸??
        PlayHitEffects();
        ApplySimpleReaction();

        // 5) ?덊듃?ㅽ넲(媛꾨떒)
        if (hitStop > 0f) _hitStopRemain = hitStop;
    }

    // ================================ ?대? 泥섎━ ?⑥닔 ================================
    void ApplyLocalDamage(int dmg)
    {
        if (dmg <= 0) return;
        currentHP -= dmg;
        if (currentHP <= 0 && !_isDead)
        {
            _isDead = true;
            currentHP = 0;
            OnDied?.Invoke();
            if (destroyOnDeath) Destroy(gameObject);
        }
    }

    void PlayHitEffects()
    {
        if (hitVFXPrefab)
        {
            var pos = vfxSpawnPoint ? vfxSpawnPoint.position : transform.position;
            Instantiate(hitVFXPrefab, pos, Quaternion.identity);
        }
        if (hitSFX && audioSource)
        {
            audioSource.PlayOneShot(hitSFX);
        }
    }

    void ApplySimpleReaction()
    {
        if (rigidbodyForReaction && knockbackForce > 0f)
        {
            // ?뚮젅?댁뼱 ???寃?諛⑺뼢?쇰줈 媛꾨떒 諛爾먮궡湲??섑룊 湲곗?)
            // ?ㅼ젣 寃뚯엫?먯꽑 ?덊듃 諛⑺뼢 踰≫꽣瑜??뚮젅?댁뼱 ?꾩튂/怨듦꺽 諛⑺뼢?먯꽌 諛쏆븘?ㅻ뒗 寃??뺥솗??
            Vector3 dir = -transform.forward;
            dir.y = 0f;
            dir.Normalize();
            rigidbodyForReaction.AddForce(dir * knockbackForce, ForceMode.VelocityChange);
        }
    }

    // ================================ ?쇰툝由??좏떥(?붾쾭洹??곕룞) ================================
    [ContextMenu("?붾쾭洹? 釉뚮젅?댄겕 On")]
    public void Debug_BreakOn() => isBreak = true;

    [ContextMenu("?붾쾭洹? 釉뚮젅?댄겕 Off")]
    public void Debug_BreakOff() => isBreak = false;

    [ContextMenu("?붾쾭洹? HP ?꾨? ?뚮났")]
    public void Debug_FullHeal() => currentHP = maxHP;

    // ?몃? ?쒖뒪??釉뚮젅?댄겕 寃뚯씠吏, ?섏씠利??꾪솚 ???먯꽌 ?몄텧
    public void SetBreak(bool on) => isBreak = on;
}

// ==========================================
// File: FitQuadToCamera.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;
[ExecuteAlways]
public class FitQuadToCamera : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] Camera cam; [SerializeField] Transform quad; [SerializeField] float z = 1f;
    void Reset() { if (!cam) cam = Camera.main; }
    void LateUpdate()
    {
        if (!cam || !quad) return;
        quad.SetParent(cam.transform, true);
        quad.localPosition = new Vector3(0, 0, z);
        quad.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
        float h = 2f * z * Mathf.Tan(0.5f * cam.fieldOfView * Mathf.Deg2Rad);
        float w = h * cam.aspect;
        quad.localScale = new Vector3(w, h, 1f);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// File: ImageFXSignalBridge.cs
// ==========================================

using UnityEngine;

public class ImageFXSignalBridge : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] Material mat;
    // 공통 믹스(곱/더하기)
    public void SetMix(float mul, float add)
    {
        var pdma = mat.GetVector("_PixDistMulAdd");
        pdma.z = mul; pdma.w = add;
        mat.SetVector("_PixDistMulAdd", pdma);
    }
    // HSVC
    public void SetHSVC(float hue, float sat, float val, float cont)
    {
        mat.SetVector("_HSVC", new Vector4(hue, sat, val, cont));
        // HSVC는 내부에서 컬러매트릭스로 변환되도록 커스텀 인스펙터가 있지만,
        // 런타임엔 셰이더에서 _HSVC 직접 읽어 처리하는 버전일 수도 있어 셰이더 구현에 맞춰 사용.
    }
    // 커널 예시(픽셀 스텝, 프리셋 강도)
    public void SetKernelStep(float pxStep)
    {
        var pdma = mat.GetVector("_PixDistMulAdd");
        pdma.x = pxStep; pdma.y = pxStep; // 스크립트가 x=y로 유지함
        mat.SetVector("_PixDistMulAdd", pdma);
    }
}
